Instalacja odbywa się poprzez `make install CLIENT=opencode|claude|codex|all PROFILE=<profil> PROJECT=<projekt>`.

## Obowiązkowa polityka testowania

- Przed projektowaniem, dodawaniem, modyfikowaniem lub oceną testów przeczytaj [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md) i stosuj metodykę `test-gap-discovery`; wczytaj skill, jeśli jest dostępny. Gdy jest niedostępny, zgłoś to i nadal stosuj metodykę opisaną w tym dokumencie, zamiast zastępować ją dowolnym podejściem.
- Zmiana zachowania aplikacji wymaga adekwatnej ochrony testowej i wykonania wymaganej weryfikacji zgodnie z `docs/testing.md`. Obowiązuje to również bez Brain i handoffu.
- Brak środowiska, uprawnień lub możliwości wykonania wymaganych testów blokuje weryfikację; nie oznacza sukcesu. Zatrzymaj pracę i zgłoś blokadę, zachowując informację o wykonanych zmianach.
- Nie osłabiaj testów, nie pomijaj wymaganych kontroli ani nie obchodź izolacji, aby zakończyć zadanie. Wymagana VM dotyczy testów Rust i integracji Playwright, nie lokalnych operacji produkcyjnych, takich jak instalacja profilu.

--- START OF FILE: /var/home/kpdve/it/personal/ai-profiles/README.md ---
# ai-profiles

Repozytorium profili dla narzędzi programistycznych i agentów AI. Umożliwia
instalowanie, aktualizowanie, przełączanie i walidowanie zestawów umiejętności
oraz konfiguracji dla projektów.
Wymagania: Rust/Cargo 1.85+, Git i Make na Linux/Unix. Instalator buduje
natywne narzędzia Rust; Python nie jest wymagany. Zainstalowane runtime nie
potrzebują Cargo ani dostępu do repozytorium.

## Szybki start

Uruchamiaj polecenia z katalogu sklonowanego repozytorium:

```bash
make help
make install CLIENT=opencode PROFILE=backend-go PROJECT=~/projects/my-api
make install-codex PROFILE=react PROJECT=~/projects/my-api
make status PROJECT=~/projects/my-api
make update CLIENT=codex PROJECT=~/projects/my-api
make validate
make test
```

Bez Make zbuduj binaria przez `make rust-build`, a następnie używaj
`core/rust/target/release/ai-profile help`.
Po instalacji, aktualizacji lub zmianie profilu uruchom ponownie narzędzie,
w którym korzystasz z profilu.

Wybrane skille są kopiowane niezależnie do
`.ai-profiles/<klient>/plugin/skills/`. OpenCode otrzymuje jawne źródło skills;
repozytorium nie instaluje kopii pod `.agents/skills/` ani `.claude/skills/`.
Wtyczki Claude Code i Codex wymagają natywnej rejestracji i zaufania projektu.
Profile przygotowują wybrane MCP tylko dla wskazanego klienta
na poziomie projektu. Klienci mogą wymagać restartu, zaufania projektu,
zatwierdzenia MCP lub osobnego logowania OAuth. Status potwierdza przygotowanie
konfiguracji, nie połączenie z serwerem.

`CLIENT=opencode|claude|codex|all` jest wymagany przy install/update/switch/uninstall
i pull-update. Aliasy: `install-opencode`, `install-claude`, `install-codex`.
`status` bez CLIENT pokazuje wszystkie trzy integracje, z CLIENT tylko wybraną.
Każdy klient może mieć inny profil i wersję; operacja dla jednego nie zmienia
plików ani rekordu pozostałych. Stan formatu 2: `.ai-profiles/state.json`;
lokalne nadpisania MCP: `.ai-profiles/<klient>/local.json` (plik użytkownika).

Profil WordPress domyślnie włącza Novamirę i przygotowuje bundle klienta
`.ai-profiles/<klient>/mcp/novamira/` z dołączonym do repozytorium, zweryfikowanym ZIP-em
1.12.12. Instalator nie dostarcza ani nie tworzy poświadczeń.
Utwórz w katalogu projektu `.env.novamira` z prawami `0600` i uzupełnij endpoint,
login WordPressa oraz **hasło aplikacyjne** (nie hasło wp-admin),
Zainstaluj plugin dostarczonym `install-plugin`, aktywuj go i AI Abilities ręcznie,
zbuduj dostarczony obraz Docker i zrestartuj klienta/MCP. Most działa w Dockerze,
bez hostowego Node/npm/npx. Instalator nie dodaje reguły `.gitignore` dla Novamiry:
samodzielnie zabezpiecz plik przed commitem i dostępem przez HTTP.
Pliki poświadczeń Playwright użytkownik tworzy w katalogu głównym projektu,
raporty są pod
`.ai-profiles/reports/`. Nie migrujemy automatycznie starych lokalizacji.

`install CLIENT=all` dodaje brakujących klientów, pomija zgodne profile i odrzuca
konflikt profilu przed zapisem. Pozostałe operacje `all` dotyczą tylko
zainstalowanych klientów; brak instalacji jest błędem. Kolejność: OpenCode,
Claude, Codex. Nie ma transakcji między klientami: przygotowany stan i sukcesy
pozostają zapisane także przy późniejszym błędzie aktywacji.

Starszy stan formatu 1 jest rozpoznawany bez zapisu podczas statusu. Dopiero
update/switch wybranego klienta przenosi jego konfigurację do osobnego payloadu.
Stary stan `.opencode/ai-profile-state.json` i współdzielony payload pozostają
zamrożonymi zasobami kompatybilności — nie są automatycznie usuwane, również
po odinstalowaniu. Format 2 ma pierwszeństwo, także z pustą mapą klientów.
Instalator nie instaluje aplikacji klientów, nie uruchamia Dockera ani OAuth.
Współistnienie konfiguracji nie oznacza bezpiecznej równoległej edycji plików
aplikacji przez agentów ani obsługi równoległych instalatorów.

### Pozostałe instrukcje

- [Zarządzanie profilami, wersjami i instalacjami](docs/profile-management.md)
- [Testy Rust i Playwright w trwałej VM Debian 13 (Incus)](docs/testing.md)
- [MCP: rejestr, konfiguracja lokalna, adapter i launchery](mcp/README.md)
- [Instrukcja profilu WordPress](profiles/wordpress/README.md)

Każdy profil może dostarczać własny plik README z instrukcjami projektowymi.
README profilu WordPress jest samodzielną instrukcją instalowaną wraz z profilem.

Testy (`make test`, `make test-playwright`, `make validate-rust-migration`,
`make test-all`) wymagają osobno przygotowanego hosta Linux amd64 z Incus,
QEMU/KVM, siecią/pulą storage i rsync; nie wymagają hostowego Cargo, Node ani
Dockera. `make test-vm-up` przygotowuje VM (4 CPU, 8 GiB RAM, dysk 64 GiB),
`make test-vm-stop` zatrzymuje ją z zachowaniem cache. Testy zawsze wykonują się
ponownie; logi i raporty trafiają do `.test-vm/artifacts/`. Szczegóły wymagań,
zasobów i jawnego re-provisioningu są w instrukcji testowania powyżej.

--- START OF FILE: /var/home/kpdve/it/personal/ai-profiles/core/rust/src/codec.rs ---
use std::{fs, path::Path};

use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::Value;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use crate::error::CodecError;

pub(crate) fn sha(content: &[u8]) -> String {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(content);
    let mut hex = String::with_capacity(digest.len() * 2);
    for byte in digest {
        use std::fmt::Write as _;
        write!(&mut hex, "{byte:02x}").expect("writing to String cannot fail");
    }
    hex
}

/// Serialize JSON with the Python implementation's two-space indentation, UTF-8,
/// insertion order, and final line feed.
pub(crate) fn json_bytes<T: Serialize + ?Sized>(value: &T) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CodecError> {
    let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(value)?;
    bytes.push(b'\n');
    Ok(bytes)
}

pub(crate) fn read_json(path: &Path) -> Result<Value, CodecError> {
    let contents = fs::read(path).map_err(|source| CodecError::Read {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })?;
    serde_json::from_slice(&contents).map_err(|source| CodecError::Parse {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })
}

use std::path::PathBuf;

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum ModelError {
    #[error("Invalid profile name: {0:?}")]
    InvalidProfileName(String),
    #[error("Invalid MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH version: {0:?}")]
    InvalidVersion(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum CodecError {
    #[error("Failed to read JSON from {path}: {source}")]
    Read {
        path: PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
    #[error("Invalid JSON in {path}: {source}")]
    Parse {
        path: PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: serde_json::Error,
    },
    #[error("Failed to serialize JSON: {0}")]
    Serialize(#[from] serde_json::Error),
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum FilesystemError {
    #[error("Invalid managed path: {0}")]
    InvalidManagedPath(String),
    #[error("Operation refused because of symlink: {0}")]
    Symlink(PathBuf),
    #[error("Conflict: managed file was changed or removed: {relative}. Back up and restore the managed version before updating or removing it.")]
    ManagedFileChanged { relative: String },
    #[error("Conflict with existing unmanaged file: {0}")]
    UnmanagedFileExists(String),
    #[error("Filesystem error for {path}: {source}")]
    Io {
        path: PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum ProcessError {
    #[error("Failed to start process: {source}")]
    Spawn {
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
    #[error("Process timed out")]
    Timeout,
    #[error("Process was cancelled")]
    Cancelled,
    #[error("Process communication error: {source}")]
    Io {
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
    #[error("Process stream handling thread panicked")]
    StreamThread,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum RepositoryError {
    #[error("Could not find an ai-profiles checkout at {0}")]
    CheckoutNotFound(PathBuf),
    #[error("Invalid profile source directory: {0}")]
    InvalidSourceRoot(PathBuf),
    #[error("Invalid profile.json: {0}")]
    InvalidMetadata(String),
    #[error("Filesystem error for {path}: {source}")]
    Io {
        path: PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
    #[error(transparent)]
    Codec(#[from] CodecError),
    #[error(transparent)]
    Model(#[from] ModelError),
    #[error(transparent)]
    Process(#[from] ProcessError),
}

    GitignoreNotFile,
    #[error("opencode.json must contain a JSON object")]
    ConfigMustBeObject,
    #[error("opencode.json: mcp must be an object")]
    McpMustBeObject,
    #[error("opencode.json: instructions must be an array")]
    InstructionsMustBeArray,

    Io {
        path: PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },

    #[error(transparent)]

}

    BackupDirectoryInsideProject,
    #[error("Another import for this project is already running")]
    RestoreAlreadyRunning,
    #[error("The source or target changed after confirmation; run the plan again")]
    RestorePlanChanged,
    #[error("The source file changed during copying")]
    IncomingChanged,
    #[error("The local database backup does not match the confirmed target")]
    BackupMismatch,
    #[error("The safety file changed after confirmation; it will not be overwritten")]
    SafetyChanged,
    #[error("Invalid topology in the imported database")]
    ImportedTopologyMismatch,
    #[error("Cancelled; the database was not changed")]
    RestoreCancelled,
    #[error("Failed to prepare the private backup directory")]
    BackupDirectory,
    #[error("Failed to lock the backup directory")]
    BackupLock,
    #[error("Failed to prepare restore files")]
    RestoreIo,
    #[error("Missing input to confirm the restore")]
    ConfirmationInput,
    #[error("Import aborted. No automatic rollback was performed. Restore the backup manually.")]
    RecoveryRequired,
    #[error(transparent)]
    Filesystem(#[from] crate::error::FilesystemError),
}

--- START OF FILE: /var/home/kpdve/it/personal/ai-profiles/core/rust/src/filesystem.rs ---
use std::{
    fs,
    io::Write,
    os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt,
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
};

use serde_json::Value;

use crate::{codec::sha, error::FilesystemError};

pub(crate) fn file_path(project: &Path, relative: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, FilesystemError> {
    let relative_path = Path::new(relative);
    if relative_path.is_absolute()
        || relative_path
            .components()
            .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::ParentDir | Component::Prefix(_)))
    {
        return Err(FilesystemError::InvalidManagedPath(relative.to_owned()));
    }

    let mut path = project.to_owned();
    for component in relative_path.components() {
        if let Component::Normal(part) = component {
            path.push(part);
            if fs::symlink_metadata(&path).is_ok_and(|metadata| metadata.file_type().is_symlink()) {
                return Err(FilesystemError::Symlink(path));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(project.join(relative_path))
}

/// Validate ownership and every destination before the caller starts writing.
pub(crate) fn check_files<'a>(
    project: &Path,
    old_state: Option<&Value>,
    next_files: impl IntoIterator<Item = &'a str>,
) -> Result<(), FilesystemError> {
    let owned = old_state
        .and_then(|state| state.get("files"))
        .and_then(Value::as_object);

    if let Some(owned) = owned {
        for (relative, expected_hash) in owned {
            let path = file_path(project, relative)?;
            let expected_hash = expected_hash.as_str().unwrap_or_default();
            let actual = fs::metadata(&path)
                .ok()
                .filter(std::fs::Metadata::is_file)
                .and_then(|_| fs::read(&path).ok())
                .map(|contents| sha(&contents));
            if actual.as_deref() != Some(expected_hash) {
                return Err(FilesystemError::ManagedFileChanged {
                    relative: relative.clone(),
                });
            }
        }
    }

    for relative in next_files {
        let path = file_path(project, relative)?;
        let exists = match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
            Ok(_) => true,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => false,
            Err(source) => {
                return Err(FilesystemError::Io { path, source });
            }
        };
        if exists && !owned.is_some_and(|files| files.contains_key(relative)) {
            return Err(FilesystemError::UnmanagedFileExists(relative.to_owned()));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn write_bytes(path: &Path, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<(), FilesystemError> {
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|source| FilesystemError::Io {
            path: parent.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    }
    fs::write(path, contents).map_err(|source| FilesystemError::Io {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })
}

pub(crate) fn write_atomic(path: &Path, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<(), FilesystemError> {
    let write = || -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
        let parent = path
            .parent()
            .ok_or_else(|| std::io::Error::other("missing parent"))?;
        fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        let mut temporary = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new_in(parent)?;
        temporary.write_all(contents)?;
        temporary.as_file().sync_all()?;
        temporary.persist(path).map_err(|error| error.error)?;
        Ok(())
    };
    write().map_err(|source| FilesystemError::Io {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })
}

/// Replace the executable by rename so an already running runtime can finish.
pub(crate) fn write_executable(path: &Path, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<(), FilesystemError> {
    let write = || -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
        let parent = path.parent().ok_or_else(|| {
            std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "missing parent")
        })?;
        fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        let mut temporary = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new_in(parent)?;
        temporary.write_all(contents)?;
        temporary
            .as_file()
            .set_permissions(fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755))?;
        temporary.persist(path).map_err(|error| error.error)?;
        Ok(())
    };
    write().map_err(|source| FilesystemError::Io {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })
}

pub(crate) fn cleanup_empty_directories(
    project: &Path,
    paths: &[String],
) -> Result<(), FilesystemError> {
    for relative in paths {
        let managed = file_path(project, relative)?;
        let mut directory = managed.parent().map(Path::to_owned);
        while let Some(path) = directory {
            if path == project || path == project.join(".opencode") {
                break;
            }
            match fs::remove_dir(&path) {
                Ok(()) => directory = path.parent().map(Path::to_owned),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    }
    if paths.iter().any(|path| path.starts_with(".opencode/")) {
        let opencode = project.join(".opencode");
        let _ = fs::remove_dir(opencode);
    }
    Ok(())
}

use crate::model::Client;

/// Legacy assets are frozen; only exclusive catalogs/configuration are owned.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum Layout {
    Legacy,
    Isolated(Client),
}

pub(crate) const STATE: &str = ".ai-profiles/state.json";
pub(crate) const LEGACY_STATE: &str = ".opencode/ai-profile-state.json";
pub(crate) const LEGACY_LOCAL: &str = ".opencode/ai-profile.local.json";

impl Layout {
    pub(crate) fn from_state(client: Client, state: Option<&serde_json::Value>) -> Self {
        if state.and_then(|v| v.get("layout")).and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some("legacy") {
            Self::Legacy
        } else {
            Self::Isolated(client)
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn root(self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Legacy => ".opencode/ai-profile".into(),
            Self::Isolated(client) => format!(".ai-profiles/{}", client.as_str()),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn plugin(self) -> String {
        format!("{}/plugin", self.root())
    }
    pub(crate) fn skills(self) -> String {
        format!("{}/skills", self.plugin())
    }
    pub(crate) fn scripts(self) -> String {
        format!("{}/scripts", self.root())
    }
    pub(crate) fn readme(self) -> String {
        format!("{}/README.md", self.root())
    }
    pub(crate) fn adapter(self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Legacy => ".opencode/ai-profile-mcp".into(),
            Self::Isolated(_) => format!("{}/ai-profile-mcp", self.root()),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn local(self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Legacy => ".opencode/ai-profile.local.json".into(),
            Self::Isolated(_) => format!("{}/local.json", self.root()),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn mcp(self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Legacy => ".opencode/mcp".into(),
            Self::Isolated(_) => format!("{}/mcp", self.root()),
        }
    }

    /// Only recognized repository-managed asset paths; never arbitrary strings.
    pub(crate) fn managed_path(self, path: &str) -> String {
        for (prefix, replacement) in [
            (".opencode/ai-profile/", format!("{}/", self.root())),
            (".opencode/mcp/", format!("{}/", self.mcp())),
        ] {
            if let Some(suffix) = path.strip_prefix(prefix) {
                return format!("{replacement}{suffix}");
            }
        }
        if path == ".opencode/ai-profile-mcp" {
            self.adapter()
        } else if path == ".opencode/ai-profile.local.json" {
            self.local()
        } else {
            path.to_owned()
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn render_text(self, text: &str, client: Client) -> String {
        [
            ("{{AI_PROFILE_ROOT}}", self.root()),
            ("{{AI_PROFILE_MCP}}", self.mcp()),
            ("{{AI_PROFILE_LOCAL}}", self.local()),
            ("{{AI_PROFILE_CLIENT}}", client.as_str().into()),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .fold(text.to_owned(), |text, (key, value)| {
            text.replace(key, &value)
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn render(self, text: &str) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::Legacy => text.to_owned(),
            Self::Isolated(client) => self
                .render_text(text, client)
                .replace(".opencode/ai-profile.local.json", &self.local())
                .replace(".opencode/ai-profile-mcp", &self.adapter())
                .replace(".opencode/ai-profile/", &format!("{}/", self.root()))
                .replace(".opencode/mcp/", &format!("{}/", self.mcp())),
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) mod archive;
pub mod runtime;

pub(crate) const COMMIT: &str = "64876fb1a4cb81bf51564c46eb60401097c067cc";
pub(crate) const ARCHIVE_URL: &str =
    "https://codeload.github.com/MariaDB/mcp/tar.gz/64876fb1a4cb81bf51564c46eb60401097c067cc";
pub(crate) const ARCHIVE_SHA256: &str =
    "0730bd3dd546bce18fb3cb4f940fd79980d6114b89909c72da1ebefc1d5fbbc0";
pub(crate) const IMAGE: &str = "ai-profiles/mariadb-mcp:64876fb1a4cb81bf51564c46eb60401097c067cc";
pub(crate) const SOURCE_LABEL: &str = "ai-profiles.mariadb.source";
pub(crate) const PLATFORM: &str = "linux/amd64";
pub(crate) const MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES: usize = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
pub(crate) const ARCHIVE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: u64 = 60;
pub(crate) const REQUIRED: [&str; 5] = ["DB_HOST", "DB_PORT", "DB_NAME", "DB_USER", "DB_PASSWORD"];

];

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum MariaDbError {
    #[error("Missing required MariaDB setting: {0}")]
    MissingSetting(&'static str),
    #[error("DB_PORT must be a number between 1 and 65535")]
    InvalidPort,
    #[error("{0} must be true or false")]
    InvalidBoolean(&'static str),
    #[error("MariaDB MCP source checksum mismatch; image was not built")]
    ChecksumMismatch,
    #[error("Unsafe MariaDB MCP source archive")]
    UnsafeArchive,
    #[error("MariaDB MCP source archive has no Dockerfile")]
    MissingDockerfile,
    #[error("Could not download MariaDB MCP source archive")]
    Download,
    #[error("Invalid MariaDB MCP source archive")]
    InvalidArchive,
    #[error("A running Linux Docker daemon is required")]
    DockerUnavailable,
    #[error("MariaDB MCP image build failed; check Docker build progress on stderr")]
    ImageBuildFailed,
    #[error("MariaDB MCP Docker command failed")]
    DockerCommand,
    #[error("MariaDB MCP could not determine a writable cache directory")]
    CacheUnavailable,
    #[error("MariaDB MCP Docker exec failed")]
    Exec,
    #[error("Błąd systemu plików dla {path}: {source}")]
    Io {
        path: std::path::PathBuf,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
}

--- START OF FILE: /var/home/kpdve/it/personal/ai-profiles/core/rust/src/mariadb/archive.rs ---
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{self, Cursor, Read, Write},
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
    time::Duration,
};

use flate2::read::GzDecoder;
use tar::Archive;

use crate::{
    codec::sha,
    mariadb::{
        MariaDbError, ARCHIVE_SHA256, ARCHIVE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, ARCHIVE_URL, COMMIT,
        MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES,
    },
};

pub(crate) trait SourceDownloader {
    fn download(
        &self,
        url: &str,
        timeout: Duration,
        maximum_bytes: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MariaDbError>;
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub(crate) struct HttpSourceDownloader;

impl SourceDownloader for HttpSourceDownloader {
    fn download(
        &self,
        url: &str,
        timeout: Duration,
        maximum_bytes: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MariaDbError> {
        let config = ureq::Agent::config_builder()
            .timeout_global(Some(timeout))
            .build();
        let agent: ureq::Agent = config.into();
        let response = agent.get(url).call().map_err(|_| MariaDbError::Download)?;
        let reader = response.into_body().into_reader();
        let limit = u64::try_from(maximum_bytes)
            .unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
            .saturating_add(1);
        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        reader
            .take(limit)
            .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
            .map_err(|_| MariaDbError::Download)?;
        Ok(bytes)
    }
}

pub(crate) fn fetch_source(
    destination: &Path,
    downloader: &impl SourceDownloader,
) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    let archive = downloader.download(
        ARCHIVE_URL,
        Duration::from_secs(ARCHIVE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS),
        MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES,
    )?;
    if archive.len() > MAX_ARCHIVE_BYTES {
        return Err(MariaDbError::ChecksumMismatch);
    }
    if sha(&archive) != ARCHIVE_SHA256 {
        return Err(MariaDbError::ChecksumMismatch);
    }
    extract_verified_archive(&archive, destination)
}

fn extract_verified_archive(archive_bytes: &[u8], destination: &Path) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    let decoder = GzDecoder::new(Cursor::new(archive_bytes));
    let mut archive = Archive::new(decoder);
    let entries = archive
        .entries()
        .map_err(|_| MariaDbError::InvalidArchive)?;
    let expected_root = format!("mcp-{COMMIT}");

    for entry in entries {
        let mut entry = entry.map_err(|_| MariaDbError::InvalidArchive)?;
        let entry_type = entry.header().entry_type();
        if !entry_type.is_file() && !entry_type.is_dir() {
            return Err(MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive);
        }
        let entry_path = entry
            .path()
            .map_err(|_| MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive)?
            .into_owned();
        let parts = safe_archive_parts(&entry_path, &expected_root)?;
        let mut target = destination.to_owned();
        for part in parts.iter().skip(1) {
            target.push(part);
        }

        if entry_type.is_dir() {
            fs::create_dir_all(&target).map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
                path: target,
                source,
            })?;
        } else {
            let parent = target
                .parent()
                .ok_or(MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive)?
                .to_owned();
            fs::create_dir_all(&parent).map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
                path: parent,
                source,
            })?;
            let mut output = fs::OpenOptions::new()
                .write(true)
                .create(true)
                .truncate(true)
                .open(&target)
                .map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
                    path: target.clone(),
                    source,
                })?;
            io::copy(&mut entry, &mut output).map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
                path: target.clone(),
                source,
            })?;
            output.flush().map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
                path: target,
                source,
            })?;
        }
    }

    if !destination.join("Dockerfile").is_file() {
        return Err(MariaDbError::MissingDockerfile);
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn safe_archive_parts(path: &Path, expected_root: &str) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, MariaDbError> {
    if path.is_absolute()
        || path
            .components()
            .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::ParentDir | Component::Prefix(_)))
    {
        return Err(MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive);
    }
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    for component in path.components() {
        match component {
            Component::Normal(part) => parts.push(PathBuf::from(part)),
            Component::CurDir => {}
            Component::ParentDir | Component::RootDir | Component::Prefix(_) => {
                return Err(MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive);
            }
        }
    }
    if parts.first().and_then(|part| part.to_str()) != Some(expected_root)
        || parts.iter().any(|part| part.as_os_str().is_empty())
    {
        return Err(MariaDbError::UnsafeArchive);
    }
    Ok(parts)
}

use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    env,
    ffi::OsString,
    fs::{self, OpenOptions},
    os::unix::process::CommandExt,
    path::PathBuf,
    process::Command,
    time::Duration,
};

use fs2::FileExt;

use crate::{
    mariadb::{
        archive::{fetch_source, HttpSourceDownloader, SourceDownloader},
        MariaDbError, COMMIT, CONTAINER_ENV, IMAGE, PLATFORM, SOURCE_LABEL,
    },
    process::{
        CancellationToken, CommandSpec, ProcessRunner, StdinMode, StreamMode, SystemProcessRunner,
    },
};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct DatabasePort(u16);

impl DatabasePort {
    pub(crate) fn get(self) -> u16 {
        self.0
    }
}

/// Validated settings retain environment values for the later runtime stage.
/// Deliberately does not implement `Debug`, because it contains credentials.
pub(crate) struct ValidatedEnvironment {
    pub(crate) port: DatabasePort,
    pub(crate) read_only: bool,
    pub(crate) block_sensitive_show: bool,
    pub(crate) values: HashMap<String, String>,
}

pub(crate) fn validate_environment(
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
) -> Result<ValidatedEnvironment, MariaDbError> {
    for key in super::REQUIRED {
        if !environment.get(key).is_some_and(|value| !value.is_empty()) {
            return Err(MariaDbError::MissingSetting(key));
        }
    }

    let port_value = environment
        .get("DB_PORT")
        .ok_or(MariaDbError::MissingSetting("DB_PORT"))?;
    if !port_value.is_ascii() || !port_value.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit()) {
        return Err(MariaDbError::InvalidPort);
    }
    let port = port_value
        .parse::<u16>()
        .ok()
        .filter(|port| *port != 0)
        .map(DatabasePort)
        .ok_or(MariaDbError::InvalidPort)?;

    let read_only = parse_bool(environment, "MCP_READ_ONLY")?;
    let block_sensitive_show = parse_bool(environment, "MCP_BLOCK_SENSITIVE_SHOW")?;
    Ok(ValidatedEnvironment {
        port,
        read_only,
        block_sensitive_show,
        values: environment.clone(),
    })
}

fn parse_bool(
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
    key: &'static str,
) -> Result<bool, MariaDbError> {
    match environment.get(key).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("false") {
        "true" => Ok(true),
        "false" => Ok(false),
        _ => Err(MariaDbError::InvalidBoolean(key)),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn docker_environment(environment: &HashMap<String, String>) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    const ALLOWED: [&str; 14] = [
        "PATH",
        "HOME",
        "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR",
        "XDG_CONFIG_HOME",
        "TMPDIR",
        "HTTP_PROXY",
        "HTTPS_PROXY",
        "NO_PROXY",
        "http_proxy",
        "https_proxy",
        "no_proxy",
        "SSL_CERT_FILE",
        "SSL_CERT_DIR",
        "SSH_AUTH_SOCK",
    ];
    environment
        .iter()
        .filter(|(key, _)| ALLOWED.contains(&key.as_str()) || key.starts_with("DOCKER_"))
        .map(|(key, value)| (key.clone(), value.clone()))
        .collect()
}

#[allow(dead_code)] // Direct production implementation; launch uses the injectable process boundary.
pub(crate) fn image_available(environment: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Result<bool, MariaDbError> {
    image_available_with(environment, &SystemProcessRunner)
}

fn image_available_with(
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
) -> Result<bool, MariaDbError> {
    let format = format!("{{{{ index .Config.Labels \"{SOURCE_LABEL}\" }}}}");
    let output = run_docker(
        runner,
        ["image", "inspect", "--format", format.as_str(), IMAGE],
        environment,
        Duration::from_secs(30),
        StreamMode::Capture,
        StreamMode::Capture,
    )?;
    Ok(output.status.success() && String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim() == COMMIT)
}

#[allow(dead_code)] // Direct production implementation; launch uses the injectable process boundary.
pub(crate) fn ensure_image(environment: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    ensure_image_with(environment, &SystemProcessRunner, &HttpSourceDownloader)
}

pub(crate) fn ensure_image_with(
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
    downloader: &impl SourceDownloader,
) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    let build_environment = docker_environment(environment);
    if image_available_with(&build_environment, runner)? {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let daemon = run_docker(
        runner,
        ["info", "--format", "{{.OSType}}"],
        &build_environment,
        Duration::from_secs(30),
        StreamMode::Capture,
        StreamMode::Capture,
    )?;
    if !daemon.status.success() || String::from_utf8_lossy(&daemon.stdout).trim() != "linux" {
        return Err(MariaDbError::DockerUnavailable);
    }

    let cache = cache_directory(environment)?
        .join("ai-profiles")
        .join("mariadb");
    fs::create_dir_all(&cache).map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
        path: cache.clone(),
        source,
    })?;
    let lock_path = cache.join(format!("{COMMIT}.lock"));
    let lock = OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .append(true)
        .open(&lock_path)
        .map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
            path: lock_path.clone(),
            source,
        })?;
    FileExt::lock_exclusive(&lock).map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
        path: lock_path,
        source,
    })?;
    if image_available_with(&build_environment, runner)? {
        return Ok(());
    }

    eprintln!(
        "MariaDB MCP: building pinned image; first start can take several minutes and several GB of disk space. Progress is on stderr."
    );
    let temporary = tempfile::Builder::new()
        .prefix("ai-profiles-mariadb-")
        .tempdir()
        .map_err(|source| MariaDbError::Io {
            path: env::temp_dir(),
            source,
        })?;
    fetch_source(temporary.path(), downloader)?;

    let label = format!("{SOURCE_LABEL}={COMMIT}");
    let build_args = vec![
        OsString::from("build"),
        OsString::from("--platform"),
        OsString::from(PLATFORM),
        OsString::from("--label"),
        OsString::from(label),
        OsString::from("--tag"),
        OsString::from(IMAGE),
        temporary.path().as_os_str().to_owned(),
    ];
    let built = run_docker_os(
        runner,
        build_args,
        &build_environment,
        Duration::from_secs(3600),
        StreamMode::RedirectToStderr,
        StreamMode::Inherit,
    )?;
    if !built.status.success() || !image_available_with(&build_environment, runner)? {
        return Err(MariaDbError::ImageBuildFailed);
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn launch(environment: HashMap<String, String>) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    launch_with(environment, &SystemProcessRunner, &HttpSourceDownloader)
}

fn launch_with(
    environment: HashMap<String, String>,
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
    downloader: &impl SourceDownloader,
) -> Result<(), MariaDbError> {
    let mut settings = validate_environment(&environment)?;
    let _validated_port = settings.port.get();
    ensure_image_with(&settings.values, runner, downloader)?;

    let read_only_default = settings.read_only.to_string();
    settings
        .values
        .entry("MCP_READ_ONLY".to_owned())
        .or_insert(read_only_default);
    let block_sensitive_default = settings.block_sensitive_show.to_string();
    settings
        .values
        .entry("MCP_BLOCK_SENSITIVE_SHOW".to_owned())
        .or_insert(block_sensitive_default);

    let network = settings
        .values
        .get("MARIADB_MCP_NETWORK")
        .map(String::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("host");
    let mut args = vec![
        OsString::from("run"),
        OsString::from("--interactive"),
        OsString::from("--rm"),
        OsString::from("--platform"),
        OsString::from(PLATFORM),
        OsString::from(format!("--network={network}")),
    ];
    for key in CONTAINER_ENV {
        if settings.values.contains_key(key) {
            args.extend([OsString::from("--env"), OsString::from(key)]);
        }
    }
    args.extend([
        OsString::from(IMAGE),
        OsString::from("python"),
        OsString::from("src/server.py"),
        OsString::from("--transport"),
        OsString::from("stdio"),
    ]);

    let mut command = Command::new("docker");
    command.args(args).envs(&settings.values);
    let _exec_error = command.exec();
    Err(MariaDbError::Exec)
}

fn run_docker<const N: usize>(
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
    args: [&str; N],
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
    timeout: Duration,
    stdout: StreamMode,
    stderr: StreamMode,
) -> Result<crate::process::CommandOutput, MariaDbError> {
    run_docker_os(
        runner,
        args.into_iter().map(OsString::from).collect(),
        environment,
        timeout,
        stdout,
        stderr,
    )
}

fn run_docker_os(
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
    args: Vec<OsString>,
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
    timeout: Duration,
    stdout: StreamMode,
    stderr: StreamMode,
) -> Result<crate::process::CommandOutput, MariaDbError> {
    let process_environment = environment
        .iter()
        .map(|(key, value)| (OsString::from(key), OsString::from(value)))
        .collect();
    runner
        .run(CommandSpec {
            program: OsString::from("docker"),
            args,
            cwd: None,
            env: process_environment,
            clear_env: true,
            stdin: StdinMode::Null,
            stdout,
            stderr,
            timeout: Some(timeout),
            cancellation: CancellationToken::new(),
        })
        .map_err(|_| MariaDbError::DockerCommand)
}

fn cache_directory(environment: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Result<PathBuf, MariaDbError> {
    if let Some(path) = environment
        .get("XDG_CACHE_HOME")
        .filter(|path| !path.is_empty())
    {
        return Ok(PathBuf::from(path));
    }
    environment
        .get("HOME")
        .filter(|home| !home.is_empty())
        .map(|home| PathBuf::from(home).join(".cache"))
        .ok_or(MariaDbError::CacheUnavailable)
}

pub fn main() -> i32 {
    let environment = env::vars_os()
        .filter_map(|(key, value)| Some((key.into_string().ok()?, value.into_string().ok()?)))
        .collect();
    match launch(environment) {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) if is_generic_failure(&error) => {
            eprintln!(
                "MariaDB MCP startup failed: check Docker, network access, cache and disk space"
            );
            1
        }
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("MariaDB MCP startup failed: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

fn is_generic_failure(error: &MariaDbError) -> bool {
    matches!(
        error,
        MariaDbError::Download
            | MariaDbError::InvalidArchive
            | MariaDbError::DockerCommand
            | MariaDbError::CacheUnavailable
            | MariaDbError::Exec
            | MariaDbError::Io { .. }
    )
}

use std::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, HashSet},
    fs,
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
    str::FromStr,
};

use serde_json::{Map, Number, Value};

use crate::{
    codec::read_json,
    error::McpConfigError,
    filesystem::file_path,
    mcp::{
        McpAccessMode, McpName, McpOAuth, McpProtocol, McpServerDefinition, McpTimeout,
        McpTransport, PositiveInteger, TimeoutPhase,
    },
    model::{ProfileName, RepositoryContext},
};

const TIMEOUT_FIELDS: [&str; 3] = ["startup", "catalog", "execution"];
#[cfg(test)]
const LOCAL_CONFIG: &str = ".opencode/ai-profile.local.json";
const NATIVE_LAUNCHER: &str = ".opencode/ai-profile-mcp";
const OVERRIDE_FIELDS: [&str; 14] = [
    "type",
    "command",
    "environment",
    "env_file",
    "enabled",
    "disabled",
    "cwd",
    "url",
    "headers",
    "oauth",
    "timeout",
    "codemode",
    "protocol",
    "access_mode",
];

impl McpName {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, McpConfigError> {
        ProfileName::from_str(value).map_err(|_| McpConfigError::InvalidName)?;
        Ok(Self(value.to_owned()))
    }

    pub(crate) fn as_str(&self) -> &str {
        &self.0
    }
}

pub(crate) fn mcp_definition(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    name: &str,
) -> Result<McpServerDefinition, McpConfigError> {
    let name = McpName::parse(name)?;
    let relative = format!("mcp/servers/{}.json", name.as_str());
    let path = file_path(&context.sources, &relative)?;
    if !path.is_file() {
        return Err(McpConfigError::MissingDefinition {
            name: name.as_str().to_owned(),
        });
    }
    let entry = read_json(&path)?;
    parse_server(&name, entry, None)
}

pub(crate) fn desired_mcp(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<McpServerDefinition>, McpConfigError> {
    let manifest_path = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("profiles/{profile}/mcp.json"))?;
    let manifest = read_json(&manifest_path)?;
    let Some(object) = manifest.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
        });
    };
    if object.len() != 1 || !object.get("servers").is_some_and(Value::is_array) {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
        });
    }
    let Some(server_names) = object.get("servers").and_then(Value::as_array) else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
        });
    };
    let mut seen = HashSet::with_capacity(server_names.len());
    let mut definitions = Vec::with_capacity(server_names.len());
    for value in server_names {
        let Some(name) = value.as_str() else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidName);
        };
        let parsed_name = McpName::parse(name)?;
        if !seen.insert(parsed_name.as_str().to_owned()) {
            return Err(McpConfigError::DuplicateName {
                profile: profile.to_owned(),
                name: name.to_owned(),
            });
        }
        definitions.push(mcp_definition(context, parsed_name.as_str())?);
    }
    Ok(definitions)
}

pub(crate) fn validate_servers(
    servers: &Map<String, Value>,
    project: Option<&Path>,
) -> Result<Vec<McpServerDefinition>, McpConfigError> {
    servers
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, entry)| {
            let name = McpName::parse(name)?;
            parse_server(&name, entry.clone(), project)
        })
        .collect()
}

fn parse_server(
    name: &McpName,
    entry: Value,
    _project: Option<&Path>,
) -> Result<McpServerDefinition, McpConfigError> {
    let Some(object) = entry.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidType(name.as_str().to_owned()));
    };
    let transport = match object.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        Some("local") => McpTransport::Local,
        Some("remote") => McpTransport::Remote,
        _ => return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidType(name.as_str().to_owned())),
    };

    let command = match transport {
        McpTransport::Local => {
            let Some(values) = object.get("command").and_then(Value::as_array) else {
                return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.as_str().to_owned()));
            };
            let command = values.iter().map(Value::as_str).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>();
            let Some(command) = command
                .filter(|parts| !parts.is_empty() && parts.iter().all(|part| !part.is_empty()))
            else {
                return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.as_str().to_owned()));
            };
            Some(command.into_iter().map(str::to_owned).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        }
        McpTransport::Remote => None,
    };
    let url = if transport == McpTransport::Remote {
        let Some(url) = object
            .get("url")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .filter(|url| !url.is_empty())
        else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::MissingRemoteUrl(name.as_str().to_owned()));
        };
        Some(url.to_owned())
    } else {
        None
    };

    let access_mode = match object.get("access_mode") {
        None => None,
        Some(_) if name.as_str() != "postgres" || transport != McpTransport::Local => {
            return Err(McpConfigError::AccessModeUnsupported);
        }
        Some(value) => Some(parse_access_mode(value).ok_or(McpConfigError::InvalidAccessMode)?),
    };

    if name.as_str() == "postgres" && transport == McpTransport::Local {
        let Some(command) = command.as_deref() else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.as_str().to_owned()));
        };
        split_access_mode(command)?;
    }

    let environment = string_map(object, "environment", name.as_str())?;
    for key in environment.keys() {
        if !valid_environment_name(key) {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidEnvironmentName(
                name.as_str().to_owned(),
            ));
        }
    }
    let headers = string_map(object, "headers", name.as_str())?;
    let enabled = optional_bool(object, "enabled", name.as_str())?;
    let disabled = optional_bool(object, "disabled", name.as_str())?;
    let codemode = optional_bool(object, "codemode", name.as_str())?;
    let cwd = match object.get("cwd") {
        None => None,
        Some(value) => Some(
            value
                .as_str()
                .filter(|cwd| !cwd.is_empty())
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidCwd(name.as_str().to_owned()))?
                .to_owned(),
        ),
    };
    let timeout = object
        .get("timeout")
        .map(|value| parse_timeout(value, name.as_str()))
        .transpose()?;
    let oauth = object
        .get("oauth")
        .map(|value| parse_oauth(value, name.as_str()))
        .transpose()?;
    let protocol = object
        .get("protocol")
        .map(|value| parse_protocol(value, name.as_str()))
        .transpose()?;
    let env_file = match object.get("env_file") {
        None => None,
        Some(value) => {
            if transport != McpTransport::Local {
                return Err(McpConfigError::RemoteEnvFile(name.as_str().to_owned()));
            }
            let relative = value.as_str().ok_or(McpConfigError::InvalidProjectPath)?;
            relative_project_path(relative)?;
            Some(relative.to_owned())
        }
    };

    Ok(McpServerDefinition {
        name: name.clone(),
        transport,
        command,
        url,
        environment,
        headers,
        enabled,
        disabled,
        codemode,
        cwd,
        timeout,
        oauth,
        protocol,
        access_mode,
        env_file,
        raw: entry,
    })
}

pub(crate) fn project_path(project: &Path, relative: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, McpConfigError> {
    let relative_path = relative_project_path(relative)?;
    let mut path = project.to_owned();
    for component in relative_path.components() {
        if let Component::Normal(part) = component {
            path.push(part);
            if fs::symlink_metadata(&path).is_ok_and(|metadata| metadata.file_type().is_symlink()) {
                return Err(McpConfigError::ProjectSymlink);
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(project.join(relative_path))
}

fn relative_project_path(relative: &str) -> Result<&Path, McpConfigError> {
    let relative_path = Path::new(relative);
    if relative.is_empty()
        || relative_path.is_absolute()
        || relative_path
            .components()
            .any(|part| matches!(part, Component::ParentDir | Component::Prefix(_)))
    {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidProjectPath);
    }
    Ok(relative_path)
}

pub(crate) fn definitions_to_map(definitions: &[McpServerDefinition]) -> Map<String, Value> {
    definitions
        .iter()
        .map(|definition| (definition.name.as_str().to_owned(), definition.raw.clone()))
        .collect()
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn load_local(
    project: &Path,
    servers: &Map<String, Value>,
) -> Result<Map<String, Value>, McpConfigError> {
    load_local_at(project, servers, LOCAL_CONFIG)
}

fn load_local_at(
    project: &Path,
    servers: &Map<String, Value>,
    local: &str,
) -> Result<Map<String, Value>, McpConfigError> {
    let path = project_path(project, local)?;
    if !path.exists() {
        return Ok(Map::new());
    }
    let value = read_json(&path)?;
    let Some(object) = value.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidLocalManifest);
    };
    if object.keys().any(|key| key != "mcp") {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidLocalManifest);
    }
    let overrides = match object.get("mcp") {
        None => Map::new(),
        Some(value) => value
            .as_object()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or(McpConfigError::InvalidLocalManifest)?,
    };
    for (name, entry) in &overrides {
        let Some(base) = servers.get(name) else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnmanagedOverride(name.clone()));
        };
        let Some(fields) = entry.as_object() else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidOverrideFields(name.clone()));
        };
        if name == "novamira"
            && ["env_file", "command", "cwd"]
                .iter()
                .any(|key| fields.contains_key(*key))
        {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidOverrideFields(
                "novamira: command/cwd/env_file mają stałą konfigurację".into(),
            ));
        }
        if fields
            .keys()
            .any(|field| !OVERRIDE_FIELDS.contains(&field.as_str()))
        {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidOverrideFields(name.clone()));
        }
        if let Some(transport) = fields.get("type") {
            if Some(transport) != base.get("type") {
                return Err(McpConfigError::OverrideTransportChange(name.clone()));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(overrides)
}

/// Recursively merge objects; a non-object override (including an array) replaces the base.
pub(crate) fn merge(base: &Value, override_value: &Value) -> Value {
    let (Some(base_object), Some(override_object)) = (base.as_object(), override_value.as_object())
    else {
        return override_value.clone();
    };
    let mut result = base_object.clone();
    for (key, value) in override_object {
        let merged = match result.get(key) {
            Some(previous) if previous.is_object() && value.is_object() => merge(previous, value),
            _ => value.clone(),
        };
        result.insert(key.clone(), merged);
    }
    Value::Object(result)
}

pub(crate) fn split_access_mode(
    command: &[String],
) -> Result<(Vec<String>, Option<McpAccessMode>), McpConfigError> {
    let mut remaining = Vec::with_capacity(command.len());
    let mut modes = Vec::new();
    let mut index = 0;
    while index < command.len() {
        let argument = &command[index];
        if argument == "--access-mode" {
            index += 1;
            let Some(value) = command.get(index) else {
                return Err(McpConfigError::MissingCommandAccessMode);
            };
            modes.push(value.as_str());
        } else if let Some(value) = argument.strip_prefix("--access-mode=") {
            modes.push(value);
        } else {
            remaining.push(argument.clone());
        }
        index += 1;
    }
    if modes.len() > 1 {
        return Err(McpConfigError::DuplicateCommandAccessMode);
    }
    let mode = modes
        .first()
        .map(|mode| match *mode {
            "restricted" => Ok(McpAccessMode::Restricted),
            "unrestricted" => Ok(McpAccessMode::Unrestricted),
            _ => Err(McpConfigError::InvalidCommandAccessMode),
        })
        .transpose()?;
    Ok((remaining, mode))
}

pub(crate) fn apply_access_mode(entry: &mut Value) -> Result<(), McpConfigError> {
    let object = entry
        .as_object_mut()
        .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject("postgres".to_owned()))?;
    let command = object
        .get("command")
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidCommand("postgres".to_owned()))?
        .iter()
        .map(Value::as_str)
        .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()
        .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidCommand("postgres".to_owned()))?
        .into_iter()
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let (mut command, _) = split_access_mode(&command)?;
    let access_mode = match object.get("access_mode") {
        None => McpAccessMode::Unrestricted,
        Some(value) => parse_access_mode(value).ok_or(McpConfigError::InvalidAccessMode)?,
    };
    command.push(match access_mode {
        McpAccessMode::Restricted => "--access-mode=restricted".to_owned(),
        McpAccessMode::Unrestricted => "--access-mode=unrestricted".to_owned(),
    });
    object.insert(
        "command".to_owned(),
        Value::Array(command.into_iter().map(Value::String).collect()),
    );
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) struct McpConfiguration {
    pub(crate) servers: Map<String, Value>,
    pub(crate) overrides: Map<String, Value>,
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn resolve(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Map<String, Value>,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    resolve_overrides(project, base, load_local(project, base)?)
}

pub(crate) fn resolve_at(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Map<String, Value>,
    local: &str,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    let overrides = load_local_at(project, base, local)?;
    resolve_overrides(project, base, overrides)
}

pub(crate) fn resolve_overrides(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Map<String, Value>,
    overrides: Map<String, Value>,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    let mut effective = Map::new();
    for (name, entry) in base {
        let merged = match overrides.get(name) {
            Some(override_entry) => merge(entry, override_entry),
            None => entry.clone(),
        };
        effective.insert(name.clone(), merged);
    }
    validate_servers(&effective, Some(project))?;
    if effective
        .get("postgres")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .and_then(|entry| entry.get("type"))
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        == Some("local")
    {
        let postgres = effective
            .get_mut("postgres")
            .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject("postgres".to_owned()))?;
        apply_access_mode(postgres)?;
    }
    Ok(McpConfiguration {
        servers: effective,
        overrides,
    })
}

pub(crate) fn render(effective: &Map<String, Value>) -> Result<Map<String, Value>, McpConfigError> {
    let mut rendered = effective.clone();
    for (name, entry) in &mut rendered {
        let Some(object) = entry.as_object_mut() else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject(name.clone()));
        };
        if object.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some("local") {
            for field in ["env_file", "environment", "cwd", "access_mode"] {
                object.remove(field);
            }
            object.insert(
                "command".to_owned(),
                Value::Array(vec![
                    Value::String(format!("./{NATIVE_LAUNCHER}")),
                    Value::String(name.clone()),
                ]),
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(rendered)
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn configured_mcp(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Map<String, Value>,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    configured_mcp_at(project, base, LOCAL_CONFIG, NATIVE_LAUNCHER)
}

pub(crate) fn configured_mcp_at(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Map<String, Value>,
    local: &str,
    adapter: &str,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    configured_from(project, resolve_at(project, base, local)?, adapter)
}

pub(crate) fn configured_from(
    project: &Path,
    resolved: McpConfiguration,
    adapter: &str,
) -> Result<McpConfiguration, McpConfigError> {
    let McpConfiguration {
        servers: effective,
        overrides,
    } = resolved;
    let mut rendered = render(&effective)?;
    for (name, entry) in &mut rendered {
        if effective
            .get(name)
            .and_then(Value::as_object)
            .and_then(|value| value.get("type"))
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            == Some("local")
        {
            let object = entry
                .as_object_mut()
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject(name.clone()))?;
            object.insert(
                "command".to_owned(),
                Value::Array(vec![
                    Value::String(project.join(adapter).to_string_lossy().into_owned()),
                    Value::String(name.clone()),
                ]),
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(McpConfiguration {
        servers: rendered,
        overrides,
    })
}

/// Render the resolved OpenCode-shaped entries for native project clients. The
/// adapter command is shared, while each client receives its own transport schema.
pub(crate) fn native_client_mcp(
    effective: &Map<String, Value>,
    client: &str,
) -> Result<Map<String, Value>, McpConfigError> {
    let mut result = Map::new();
    for (name, entry) in effective {
        let object = entry
            .as_object()
            .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject(name.clone()))?;
        if object.get("disabled").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(true)
            || object.get("enabled").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(false)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let transport = object.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("");
        let timeout = object.get("timeout");
        if object.get("codemode").is_some() {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnsupportedClientField {
                server: name.clone(),
                client: client.to_owned(),
                field: "codemode".into(),
            });
        }
        if object.get("protocol").is_some() {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnsupportedClientField {
                server: name.clone(),
                client: client.to_owned(),
                field: "protocol".into(),
            });
        }
        if transport == "local" && object.get("oauth").is_some() {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnsupportedClientField {
                server: name.clone(),
                client: client.to_owned(),
                field: "oauth".into(),
            });
        }
        if timeout.is_some_and(|value| value.get("catalog").is_some()) {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnsupportedClientField {
                server: name.clone(),
                client: client.to_owned(),
                field: "timeout.catalog".into(),
            });
        }
        let mut rendered = Map::new();
        if transport == "local" {
            let command = object
                .get("command")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.clone()))?;
            let command = command
                .iter()
                .map(Value::as_str)
                .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.clone()))?;
            if command.len() != 2 {
                return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidCommand(name.clone()));
            }
            rendered.insert("command".into(), Value::String(command[0].to_owned()));
            rendered.insert(
                "args".into(),
                Value::Array(vec![Value::String(command[1].to_owned())]),
            );
            if let Some(env) = object.get("environment") {
                rendered.insert("env".into(), env.clone());
            }
            if client == "claude" {
                rendered.insert("type".into(), Value::String("stdio".into()));
            }
        } else if transport == "remote" {
            rendered.insert(
                "url".into(),
                object
                    .get("url")
                    .cloned()
                    .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::MissingRemoteUrl(name.clone()))?,
            );
            if client == "claude" {
                rendered.insert("type".into(), Value::String("http".into()));
            }
            if let Some(headers) = object.get("headers") {
                rendered.insert(
                    if client == "codex" {
                        "http_headers"
                    } else {
                        "headers"
                    }
                    .into(),
                    translate_header_refs(headers),
                );
            }
            if object.get("oauth").is_some() {
                return Err(McpConfigError::UnsupportedClientField {
                    server: name.clone(),
                    client: client.to_owned(),
                    field: "oauth".into(),
                });
            }
        } else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidType(name.clone()));
        }
        if let Some(timeout) = timeout {
            let millis = |field: &str| timeout_ms(timeout, field);
            if client == "codex" {
                if let Some(ms) = millis("startup") {
                    rendered.insert("startup_timeout_sec".into(), Value::from(ms.div_ceil(1000)));
                }
                if let Some(ms) = millis("execution") {
                    rendered.insert("tool_timeout_sec".into(), Value::from(ms.div_ceil(1000)));
                }
            } else if let Some(ms) = millis("execution") {
                rendered.insert("timeout".into(), Value::from(ms));
            }
        }
        result.insert(name.clone(), Value::Object(rendered));
    }
    Ok(result)
}

fn timeout_ms(timeout: &Value, field: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    if let Some(value) = timeout.as_u64() {
        return Some(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = timeout.get(field).and_then(Value::as_u64) {
        return Some(value);
    }
    // Registry timeout numbers are serialized as exact positive integers.
    timeout
        .get(field)
        .and_then(|v| v.as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()))
}

fn translate_header_refs(value: &Value) -> Value {
    match value {
        Value::Object(map) => Value::Object(
            map.iter()
                .map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), translate_header_refs(v)))
                .collect(),
        ),
        Value::String(s) => {
            let mut result = String::new();
            let mut rest = s.as_str();
            while let Some(start) = rest.find("{env:") {
                result.push_str(&rest[..start]);
                let after = &rest[start + 5..];
                if let Some(end) = after.find('}') {
                    result.push_str("${");
                    result.push_str(&after[..end]);
                    result.push('}');
                    rest = &after[end + 1..];
                } else {
                    result.push_str(&rest[start..]);
                    rest = "";
                }
            }
            result.push_str(rest);
            Value::String(result)
        }
        _ => value.clone(),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn changed_override_paths(
    old: &Map<String, Value>,
    new: &Map<String, Value>,
    override_value: &Map<String, Value>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    collect_changed_paths(old, new, override_value, "", &mut paths);
    paths
}

fn collect_changed_paths(
    old: &Map<String, Value>,
    new: &Map<String, Value>,
    override_value: &Map<String, Value>,
    prefix: &str,
    paths: &mut Vec<String>,
) {
    for (key, override_entry) in override_value {
        let path = if prefix.is_empty() {
            key.clone()
        } else {
            format!("{prefix}.{key}")
        };
        let before = old.get(key).filter(|value| !value.is_null());
        let after = new.get(key).filter(|value| !value.is_null());
        if let (Some(override_object), Some(before_object), Some(after_object)) = (
            override_entry.as_object(),
            before.and_then(Value::as_object),
            after.and_then(Value::as_object),
        ) {
            collect_changed_paths(before_object, after_object, override_object, &path, paths);
        } else if before != after {
            paths.push(path);
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn check_mcp(
    config: &Map<String, Value>,
    old_state: Option<&Value>,
    new: &Map<String, Value>,
) -> Result<(), McpConfigError> {
    let current = match config.get("mcp") {
        None => Map::new(),
        Some(value) => value
            .as_object()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject("project configuration".to_owned()))?,
    };
    let prior = match old_state.and_then(|state| state.get("mcp")) {
        None => Map::new(),
        Some(value) => value
            .as_object()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidMcpObject("installation state".to_owned()))?,
    };
    for (name, expected) in &prior {
        if current.get(name) != Some(expected) {
            return Err(McpConfigError::ManagedMcpChanged(name.clone()));
        }
    }
    for name in new.keys() {
        if current.contains_key(name) && !prior.contains_key(name) {
            return Err(McpConfigError::UnmanagedMcpExists(name.clone()));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn string_map(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    field: &'static str,
    name: &str,
) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>, McpConfigError> {
    let Some(value) = object.get(field) else {
        return Ok(BTreeMap::new());
    };
    let Some(map) = value.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidStringMap {
            field,
            name: name.to_owned(),
        });
    };
    map.iter()
        .map(|(key, value)| {
            value
                .as_str()
                .map(|value| (key.clone(), value.to_owned()))
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidStringMap {
                    field,
                    name: name.to_owned(),
                })
        })
        .collect()
}

fn valid_environment_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut bytes = name.bytes();
    matches!(bytes.next(), Some(b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z' | b'_'))
        && bytes.all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'_')
}

fn optional_bool(
    object: &Map<String, Value>,
    field: &'static str,
    name: &str,
) -> Result<Option<bool>, McpConfigError> {
    object
        .get(field)
        .map(|value| {
            value
                .as_bool()
                .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidBoolean {
                    field,
                    name: name.to_owned(),
                })
        })
        .transpose()
}

fn parse_timeout(value: &Value, name: &str) -> Result<McpTimeout, McpConfigError> {
    if let Some(number) = value.as_number() {
        return parse_positive_integer(number)
            .map(McpTimeout::Default)
            .ok_or_else(|| McpConfigError::InvalidTimeout(name.to_owned()));
    }
    let Some(values) = value.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidTimeout(name.to_owned()));
    };
    if values
        .keys()
        .any(|key| !TIMEOUT_FIELDS.contains(&key.as_str()))
    {
        return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidTimeout(name.to_owned()));
    }
    let mut phases = BTreeMap::new();
    for (key, value) in values {
        let phase = match key.as_str() {
            "startup" => TimeoutPhase::Startup,
            "catalog" => TimeoutPhase::Catalog,
            "execution" => TimeoutPhase::Execution,
            _ => return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidTimeout(name.to_owned())),
        };
        let Some(number) = value.as_number().and_then(parse_positive_integer) else {
            return Err(McpConfigError::InvalidTimeout(name.to_owned()));
        };
        phases.insert(phase, number);
    }
    Ok(McpTimeout::Phases(phases))
}

fn parse_positive_integer(number: &Number) -> Option<PositiveInteger> {
    let value = number.to_string();
    if !value.is_empty() && value.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit()) && value != "0" {
        Some(PositiveInteger(value))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn parse_oauth(value: &Value, name: &str) -> Result<McpOAuth, McpConfigError> {
    if let Some(enabled) = value.as_bool() {
        return Ok(McpOAuth::Enabled(enabled));
    }
    if let Some(options) = value.as_object() {
        return Ok(McpOAuth::Options(options.clone()));
    }
    Err(McpConfigError::InvalidOAuth(name.to_owned()))
}

fn parse_protocol(value: &Value, name: &str) -> Result<McpProtocol, McpConfigError> {
    match value.as_str() {
        Some("legacy") => Ok(McpProtocol::Legacy),
        Some("auto") => Ok(McpProtocol::Auto),
        Some("2026-07-28") => Ok(McpProtocol::Version20260728),
        _ => Err(McpConfigError::InvalidProtocol(name.to_owned())),
    }
}

fn parse_access_mode(value: &Value) -> Option<McpAccessMode> {
    match value.as_str() {
        Some("restricted") => Some(McpAccessMode::Restricted),
        Some("unrestricted") => Some(McpAccessMode::Unrestricted),
        _ => None,
    }
}

use std::{collections::HashMap, fs, path::Path, sync::OnceLock};

use regex::Regex;
use serde_json::{Map, Value};

use crate::{error::EnvironmentError, mcp::config::project_path};

pub(crate) fn dotenv(path: &Path) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, EnvironmentError> {
    let contents = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|_| EnvironmentError::ReadEnvFile {
        filename: path
            .file_name()
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .to_string_lossy()
            .into_owned(),
    })?;
    let contents = contents.strip_prefix('\u{feff}').unwrap_or(&contents);
    let mut values = HashMap::new();
    for (index, line) in split_lines(contents).into_iter().enumerate() {
        let line_number = index + 1;
        let mut line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with('#') {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(exported) = line.strip_prefix("export ") {
            line = exported.trim_start();
        }
        let Some(separator) = line.find('=') else {
            return Err(EnvironmentError::InvalidEntry { line: line_number });
        };
        let key = line[..separator].trim();
        let mut value = line[separator + 1..].trim().to_owned();
        if !valid_environment_name(key) {
            return Err(EnvironmentError::InvalidEntry { line: line_number });
        }
        if value.starts_with(['\'', '"']) {
            let words = parse_quoted_words(&value)
                .map_err(|()| EnvironmentError::InvalidQuoting { line: line_number })?;
            if words.len() != 1 {
                return Err(EnvironmentError::InvalidValue { line: line_number });
            }
            value = words.into_iter().next().unwrap_or_default();
        } else {
            value = if value.starts_with('#') {
                String::new()
            } else {
                value
                    .split_once(" #")
                    .map_or(value.as_str(), |(before_comment, _)| before_comment)
                    .trim_end()
                    .to_owned()
            };
        }
        values.insert(key.to_owned(), value);
    }
    Ok(values)
}

pub(crate) fn environment_for(
    project: &Path,
    entry: &Map<String, Value>,
    inherited: &HashMap<String, String>,
) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, EnvironmentError> {
    let mut environment = inherited.clone();
    if let Some(env_file) = entry.get("env_file") {
        let Some(env_file) = env_file.as_str() else {
            return Err(EnvironmentError::InvalidMapping);
        };
        let path = project_path(project, env_file)?;
        environment.extend(dotenv(&path)?);
    }

    let mappings = match entry.get("environment") {
        None => None,
        Some(value) => Some(value.as_object().ok_or(EnvironmentError::InvalidMapping)?),
    };
    let mut mapped = HashMap::new();
    if let Some(mappings) = mappings {
        for (key, value) in mappings {
            let Some(value) = value.as_str() else {
                return Err(EnvironmentError::InvalidMapping);
            };
            mapped.insert(key.clone(), substitute_environment(value, &environment)?);
        }
    }
    environment.extend(mapped);
    Ok(environment)
}

fn substitute_environment(
    template: &str,
    environment: &HashMap<String, String>,
) -> Result<String, EnvironmentError> {
    static REFERENCE: OnceLock<Regex> = OnceLock::new();
    let pattern = REFERENCE.get_or_init(|| {
        Regex::new(r"\{env:([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\}")
            .expect("the fixed environment-reference regex is valid")
    });
    let mut output = String::with_capacity(template.len());
    let mut previous_end = 0;
    for capture in pattern.captures_iter(template) {
        let complete = capture.get(0).expect("capture 0 is always present");
        let name = capture
            .get(1)
            .expect("the expression has one capture")
            .as_str();
        output.push_str(&template[previous_end..complete.start()]);
        let Some(value) = environment.get(name).filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else {
            return Err(EnvironmentError::MissingVariable(name.to_owned()));
        };
        output.push_str(value);
        previous_end = complete.end();
    }
    output.push_str(&template[previous_end..]);
    Ok(output)
}

fn valid_environment_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut bytes = name.bytes();
    matches!(bytes.next(), Some(b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z' | b'_'))
        && bytes.all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'_')
}

/// Matches Python `str.splitlines()` line boundaries, including CR-only files.
fn split_lines(contents: &str) -> Vec<&str> {
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    let mut start = 0;
    let mut chars = contents.char_indices().peekable();
    while let Some((index, character)) = chars.next() {
        if !is_line_boundary(character) {
            continue;
        }
        lines.push(&contents[start..index]);
        let mut end = index + character.len_utf8();
        if character == '\r' && chars.peek().is_some_and(|(_, next)| *next == '\n') {
            let (next_index, next) = chars.next().expect("peeked line feed exists");
            end = next_index + next.len_utf8();
        }
        start = end;
    }
    if start < contents.len() {
        lines.push(&contents[start..]);
    }
    lines
}

fn is_line_boundary(character: char) -> bool {
    matches!(
        character,
        '\n' | '\r'
            | '\u{000b}'
            | '\u{000c}'
            | '\u{001c}'
            | '\u{001d}'
            | '\u{001e}'
            | '\u{0085}'
            | '\u{2028}'
            | '\u{2029}'
    )
}

/// Tokenize the quoted value like the limited POSIX `shlex.split(..., comments=True)`
/// use in Python, without invoking a shell or expanding variables.
fn parse_quoted_words(value: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, ()> {
    let mut words = Vec::new();
    let mut word = String::new();
    let mut started = false;
    let mut quote = None;
    let mut chars = value.chars().peekable();

    while let Some(character) = chars.next() {
        if let Some(quote_character) = quote {
            if character == quote_character {
                quote = None;
                started = true;
            } else if character == '\\' && quote_character == '"' {
                match chars.peek().copied() {
                    Some('"' | '\\') => {
                        word.push(chars.next().ok_or(())?);
                    }
                    _ => word.push('\\'),
                }
                started = true;
            } else {
                word.push(character);
                started = true;
            }
            continue;
        }

        match character {
            '\'' | '"' => {
                quote = Some(character);
                started = true;
            }
            '#' => break,
            '\\' => {
                word.push(chars.next().ok_or(())?);
                started = true;
            }
            character if character.is_whitespace() => {
                if started {
                    words.push(std::mem::take(&mut word));
                    started = false;
                }
            }
            _ => {
                word.push(character);
                started = true;
            }
        }
    }
    if quote.is_some() {
        return Err(());
    }
    if started {
        words.push(word);
    }
    Ok(words)
}
