//! Novamira-only stdio guard for the pinned WordPress bridge's degraded handshake.
//! Never diagnose from tools count or forward upstream error text/logs.
use std::{
    io::{self, BufRead, BufReader, Write},
    process::{Child, Command, Stdio},
    sync::{mpsc, Arc, Mutex},
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;

const DIAGNOSTIC_ID: &str = "ai-profiles/novamira/init-diagnostic";
const GENERAL: &str = "Novamira: nie udało się połączyć z WordPressem. Sprawdź adres witryny i konfigurację połączenia.";
const MAX_MESSAGE: usize = 16 * 1024 * 1024;

fn authentication_message(env_file: &str) -> String {
    format!("Novamira: WordPress odrzucił dane aplikacyjne (HTTP 401). Sprawdź WP_API_USERNAME i WP_API_PASSWORD w {env_file}. Użyj hasła aplikacyjnego WordPressa, nie hasła do panelu.")
}

fn connection_message(env_file: &str) -> String {
    format!("{GENERAL} Konfiguracja: {env_file}.")
}

fn failure(id: &Value, message: &str) {
    let response = json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":id,"error":{"code":-32603,"message":message}});
    let _ = writeln!(io::stdout().lock(), "{response}");
}

pub(super) fn configuration_failure(env_file: &str) {
    let message = format!("Novamira: utwórz w katalogu głównym projektu plik {env_file} i uzupełnij WP_API_URL, WP_API_USERNAME i WP_API_PASSWORD. Użyj hasła aplikacyjnego WordPressa, nie hasła do panelu. Sprawdź plik i konfigurację połączenia.");
    initial_failure(&message);
}

fn initial_failure(message: &str) {
    if let Ok(Some(line)) = read_message(&mut io::stdin().lock()) {
        if let Ok(request) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&line) {
            if request["method"] == "initialize" {
                failure(&request["id"], message);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn read_message(reader: &mut impl BufRead) -> io::Result<Option<Vec<u8>>> {
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    loop {
        let available = reader.fill_buf()?;
        if available.is_empty() {
            return Ok((!line.is_empty()).then_some(line));
        }
        let end = available.iter().position(|b| *b == b'\n').map(|i| i + 1);
        let count = end.unwrap_or(available.len());
        if line.len() + count > MAX_MESSAGE {
            return Err(io::Error::other("MCP message too large"));
        }
        line.extend_from_slice(&available[..count]);
        reader.consume(count);
        if end.is_some() {
            return Ok(Some(line));
        }
    }
}

enum Event {
    Message(Vec<u8>),
    Closed,
}

struct Bridge(Child);
impl Drop for Bridge {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // npx can own a shell and a Node grandchild. Terminate only this guard's
        // private process group, including on EOF/error, and reap the direct child.
        let group = format!("-{}", self.0.id());
        let _ = Command::new("kill")
            .args(["-TERM", "--", &group])
            .stdout(Stdio::null())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .status();
        // Let docker compose forward TERM and remove its --rm container before
        // escalating. Killing the CLI immediately can leave a live container.
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
        while self.0.try_wait().is_ok_and(|status| status.is_none()) && Instant::now() < deadline {
            thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }
        let _ = Command::new("kill")
            .args(["-KILL", "--", &group])
            .stdout(Stdio::null())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .status();
        let _ = self.0.kill();
        let _ = self.0.wait();
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(process: &mut Command, env_file: &str) -> bool {
    let general = connection_message(env_file);
    let Ok(Some(first)) = read_message(&mut io::stdin().lock()) else {
        return false;
    };
    let Ok(request) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&first) else {
        return false;
    };
    if request["method"] != "initialize" || request.get("id").is_none() {
        return false;
    }
    let id = &request["id"];
    // Upstream logs raw HTTP bodies even at ERROR. Do not capture or forward them,
    // and disable its optional file sink regardless of inherited debug settings.
    process
        .env_remove("LOG_FILE")
        .env("LOG_LEVEL", "-1")
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::null())
        .process_group(0);
    let Ok(child) = process.spawn() else {
        failure(id, &general);
        return false;
    };
    let mut bridge = Bridge(child);
    let input = Arc::new(Mutex::new(bridge.0.stdin.take().expect("piped stdin")));
    if input.lock().expect("stdin lock").write_all(&first).is_err() {
        failure(id, &general);
        return false;
    }
    let stdout = bridge.0.stdout.take().expect("piped stdout");
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(8);
    let signal_events = tx.clone();
    if ctrlc::set_handler(move || {
        let _ = signal_events.try_send(Event::Closed);
    })
    .is_err()
    {
        failure(id, &general);
        return false;
    }
    let output_events = tx.clone();
    thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(stdout);
        while let Ok(Some(line)) = read_message(&mut reader) {
            if output_events.send(Event::Message(line)).is_err() {
                return;
            }
        }
        let _ = output_events.send(Event::Closed);
    });
    let child_input = input.clone();
    thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut reader = io::stdin().lock();
        while let Ok(Some(line)) = read_message(&mut reader) {
            if child_input
                .lock()
                .expect("stdin lock")
                .write_all(&line)
                .is_err()
            {
                break;
            }
        }
        let _ = tx.send(Event::Closed);
    });
    let mut initialized = false;
    while let Ok(event) = rx.recv() {
        let Event::Message(line) = event else {
            if !initialized {
                failure(id, &general);
            }
            return initialized;
        };
        let Ok(response) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&line) else {
            if !initialized {
                failure(id, &general);
            }
            return false;
        };
        if !initialized && response.get("method").is_none() && response.get("id") == Some(id) {
            if response.get("error").is_some() {
                failure(id, &general);
                return false;
            }
            if response
                .pointer("/result/capabilities/experimental")
                .and_then(Value::as_object)
                .is_some_and(|e| e.contains_key("connectionFailed"))
            {
                // Upstream 0.4.0 holds the real cause in session state. tools/list
                // after failed init reads it locally; it does NOT retry HTTP.
                // The marker alone (often {}) proves no particular HTTP status.
                let diagnostic = json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":DIAGNOSTIC_ID,"method":"tools/list"});
                let _ = writeln!(input.lock().expect("stdin lock"), "{diagnostic}");
                let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
                let mut auth = false;
                while let Ok(Event::Message(line)) =
                    rx.recv_timeout(deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()))
                {
                    let Ok(detail) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&line) else {
                        break;
                    };
                    if detail["id"] == DIAGNOSTIC_ID {
                        // Fixed prefix generated by wpRequest for a non-OK HTTP
                        // response, not a substring in an untrusted server body.
                        auth = detail.pointer("/error/data/reason").and_then(Value::as_str)
                            == Some("failed")
                            && detail
                                .pointer("/error/data/detail")
                                .and_then(Value::as_str)
                                .is_some_and(|text| {
                                    text.starts_with("WordPress API error (401): ")
                                });
                        break;
                    }
                }
                let message = if auth {
                    authentication_message(env_file)
                } else {
                    general.clone()
                };
                failure(id, &message);
                return false;
            }
            initialized = true;
        }
        let mut output = io::stdout().lock();
        if output
            .write_all(&line)
            .and_then(|()| output.flush())
            .is_err()
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
    initialized
}

use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    env,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    process::Command,
};

use serde_json::Value;
use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;

use crate::{
    error::McpRuntimeError,
    mcp::{config, environment::environment_for},
};

const STATE: &str = crate::layout::LEGACY_STATE;

pub fn main() -> i32 {
    let mut args = env::args_os();
    let _program = args.next();
    let Some(server) = args.next() else {
        eprintln!("Usage: ai-profile-mcp SERVER");
        return 2;
    };
    if args.next().is_some() {
        eprintln!("Usage: ai-profile-mcp SERVER");
        return 2;
    }
    let server = match server.into_string() {
        Ok(server) => server,
        Err(_) => {
            eprintln!("MCP startup failed: Unknown local MCP server");
            return 1;
        }
    };

    match launch(&server) {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(McpRuntimeError::NovamiraRejected) => 1,
        Err(error @ McpRuntimeError::Exec) | Err(error @ McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation) => {
            eprintln!("{error}");
            1
        }
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("MCP startup failed: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

fn launch(server_name: &str) -> Result<(), McpRuntimeError> {
    let executable = env::current_exe().map_err(|_| McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
    let (project, client) = identity_from_adapter(&executable)?;
    let layout = client.map_or(
        crate::layout::Layout::Legacy,
        crate::layout::Layout::Isolated,
    );
    let state_path = config::project_path(
        &project,
        if client.is_some() {
            crate::layout::STATE
        } else {
            STATE
        },
    )?;
    let state = crate::codec::read_json(&state_path).map_err(|_| McpRuntimeError::StateRead)?;
    let state = if let Some(client) = client {
        if state.get("format").and_then(Value::as_u64) != Some(2) {
            return Err(McpRuntimeError::InvalidState);
        }
        state
            .get("clients")
            .and_then(|clients| clients.get(client.as_str()))
            .cloned()
            .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::InvalidState)?
    } else {
        state
    };
    let Some(state_object) = state.as_object() else {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::InvalidState);
    };
    if client.is_none() && !state_object.get("format").is_some_and(is_format_one) {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::InvalidState);
    }
    let Some(base) = state_object
        .get("mcp_base")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .filter(|base| !base.is_empty())
    else {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::InvalidState);
    };

    let servers = config::resolve_at(&project, base, &layout.local())?.servers;
    let Some(entry) = servers.get(server_name).and_then(Value::as_object) else {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::UnknownLocalServer);
    };
    if entry.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("local") {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::UnknownLocalServer);
    }
    if entry.get("disabled").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(true)
        || entry.get("enabled").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(false)
    {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::Disabled);
    }

    let inherited = inherited_environment();
    let env_file = entry
        .get("env_file")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("środowisku procesu (bez env_file)");
    let environment = match environment_for(&project, entry, &inherited) {
        Ok(environment) => environment,
        Err(_) if server_name == "novamira" => {
            super::novamira::configuration_failure(env_file);
            return Err(McpRuntimeError::NovamiraRejected);
        }
        Err(error) => return Err(error.into()),
    };
    if server_name == "novamira"
        && ["WP_API_URL", "WP_API_USERNAME", "WP_API_PASSWORD"]
            .iter()
            .any(|key| {
                environment
                    .get(*key)
                    .is_none_or(|value| value.trim().is_empty())
            })
    {
        super::novamira::configuration_failure(env_file);
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::NovamiraRejected);
    }
    let command = entry
        .get("command")
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::UnknownLocalServer)?;
    let Some(program) = command
        .first()
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
    else {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::UnknownLocalServer);
    };
    let args = command
        .iter()
        .skip(1)
        .map(Value::as_str)
        .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()
        .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::UnknownLocalServer)?;
    let cwd = entry
        .get("cwd")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."));
    let cwd = if cwd.is_absolute() {
        cwd
    } else {
        project.join(cwd)
    };

    // Managed launchers remain rooted in the project even with a custom cwd.
    let program = if program.starts_with(".opencode/mcp/") || program.starts_with(".ai-profiles/") {
        config::project_path(&project, program)?
    } else {
        PathBuf::from(program)
    };
    let mut process = Command::new(program);
    process.args(args).current_dir(cwd);
    apply_environment(&mut process, &project, environment);
    if server_name == "novamira" {
        return if super::novamira::run(&mut process, env_file) {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(McpRuntimeError::NovamiraRejected)
        };
    }
    let _exec_error = process.exec();
    Err(McpRuntimeError::Exec)
}

pub(crate) fn apply_environment(
    process: &mut Command,
    project: &Path,
    environment: HashMap<String, String>,
) {
    // Reserved adapter metadata wins over inherited, dotenv and mapped values.
    // Keep the native path (including non-UTF-8 bytes) scoped to the child.
    process
        .envs(environment)
        .env("AI_PROFILE_PROJECT_DIR", project);
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn project_from_adapter(executable: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, McpRuntimeError> {
    identity_from_adapter(executable).map(|(project, _)| project)
}

fn identity_from_adapter(
    executable: &Path,
) -> Result<(PathBuf, Option<crate::model::Client>), McpRuntimeError> {
    let executable = executable
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
    let parent = executable
        .parent()
        .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
    if let Some(client) = parent
        .file_name()
        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
        .and_then(crate::model::Client::parse)
    {
        let root = parent
            .parent()
            .filter(|p| p.file_name().is_some_and(|s| s == ".ai-profiles"))
            .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
        let project = root.parent().ok_or(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
        return Ok((project.to_owned(), Some(client)));
    }
    let opencode = parent
        .file_name()
        .filter(|name| *name == ".opencode")
        .map(|_| parent)
        .ok_or(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
    let project = opencode.parent().ok_or(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation)?;
    if !project.is_dir() {
        return Err(McpRuntimeError::ProjectLocation);
    }
    Ok((project.to_owned(), None))
}

fn inherited_environment() -> HashMap<String, String> {
    env::vars_os()
        .filter_map(|(key, value)| Some((key.into_string().ok()?, value.into_string().ok()?)))
        .collect()
}

fn is_format_one(value: &Value) -> bool {
    value.as_bool() == Some(true)
        || value.as_i64() == Some(1)
        || value.as_u64() == Some(1)
        || value.as_f64() == Some(1.0)
}

use std::{cmp::Ordering, path::PathBuf, str::FromStr};

use crate::error::ModelError;

#[derive(
    Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize,
)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub(crate) enum Client {
    OpenCode,
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

impl Client {
    pub(crate) const ALL: [Self; 3] = [Self::OpenCode, Self::Claude, Self::Codex];

    pub(crate) fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::OpenCode => "opencode",
            Self::Claude => "claude",
            Self::Codex => "codex",
        }
    }
}

impl FromStr for Client {
    type Err = String;

    fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
        match value {
            "opencode" => Ok(Self::OpenCode),
            "claude" => Ok(Self::Claude),
            "codex" => Ok(Self::Codex),
            _ => Err("Podaj klienta: opencode, claude lub codex".into()),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum ClientSelection {
    One(Client),
    All,
}

impl FromStr for ClientSelection {
    type Err = String;

    fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
        if value == "all" {
            Ok(Self::All)
        } else {
            value.parse().map(Self::One)
        }
    }
}

impl Client {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        value.parse().ok()
    }
}

impl ClientSelection {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
        if value == "all" {
            Ok(Self::All)
        } else {
            Client::parse(value)
                .map(Self::One)
                .ok_or_else(|| "--client wymaga opencode, claude, codex albo all".to_owned())
        }
    }
}

/// A profile identifier in lowercase kebab-case.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct ProfileName(String);

impl ProfileName {
    pub(crate) fn as_str(&self) -> &str {
        &self.0
    }
}

impl FromStr for ProfileName {
    type Err = ModelError;

    fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
        let valid = !value.is_empty()
            && value
                .bytes()
                .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || byte == b'-')
            && value.split('-').all(|part| {
                !part.is_empty() && part.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric())
            });
        if !valid {
            return Err(ModelError::InvalidProfileName(value.to_owned()));
        }
        Ok(Self(value.to_owned()))
    }
}

/// A semantic version whose decimal segments are not limited to machine integers.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct Version {
    major: String,
    minor: String,
    patch: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum VersionBump {
    Major,
    Minor,
    Patch,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum ProfileAction {
    Help,
    Create,
    Rename,
    Install,
    Update,
    Switch,
    Uninstall,
    Status,
    Validate,
    MigrateLegacy,
    Bump,
}

/// Explicit location of source assets, independent of the caller's working directory.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct RepositoryContext {
    pub(crate) sources: PathBuf,
}

impl RepositoryContext {
    pub(crate) fn from_sources(sources: PathBuf) -> Result<Self, crate::error::RepositoryError> {
        crate::repository::validate_source_root(&sources)?;
        let sources = std::fs::canonicalize(&sources).map_err(|source| {
            crate::error::RepositoryError::Io {
                path: sources,
                source,
            }
        })?;
        Ok(Self { sources })
    }

    pub(crate) fn discover() -> Result<Self, crate::error::RepositoryError> {
        let executable =
            std::env::current_exe().map_err(|source| crate::error::RepositoryError::Io {
                path: PathBuf::from("<current executable>"),
                source,
            })?;
        let start = executable
            .parent()
            .ok_or_else(|| crate::error::RepositoryError::CheckoutNotFound(executable.clone()))?;
        for candidate in start.ancestors() {
            if crate::repository::is_source_root(candidate) {
                return Self::from_sources(candidate.to_owned());
            }
        }
        Err(crate::error::RepositoryError::CheckoutNotFound(executable))
    }
}

impl Version {
    pub(crate) fn bump(&self, part: VersionBump) -> Self {
        let mut next = self.clone();
        match part {
            VersionBump::Major => {
                next.major = increment_decimal(&next.major);
                next.minor = "0".to_owned();
                next.patch = "0".to_owned();
            }
            VersionBump::Minor => {
                next.minor = increment_decimal(&next.minor);
                next.patch = "0".to_owned();
            }
            VersionBump::Patch => next.patch = increment_decimal(&next.patch),
        }
        next
    }

    pub(crate) fn as_string(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}.{}.{}", self.major, self.minor, self.patch)
    }

    fn segments(&self) -> [&str; 3] {
        [&self.major, &self.minor, &self.patch]
    }
}

impl FromStr for Version {
    type Err = ModelError;

    fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
        let mut segments = value.split('.');
        let parsed: Vec<&str> = segments.by_ref().collect();
        if parsed.len() != 3 || parsed.iter().any(|part| !valid_decimal(part)) {
            return Err(ModelError::InvalidVersion(value.to_owned()));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            major: parsed[0].to_owned(),
            minor: parsed[1].to_owned(),
            patch: parsed[2].to_owned(),
        })
    }
}

impl Ord for Version {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Ordering {
        self.segments()
            .into_iter()
            .zip(other.segments())
            .map(|(left, right)| compare_decimal(left, right))
            .find(|ordering| *ordering != Ordering::Equal)
            .unwrap_or(Ordering::Equal)
    }
}

impl PartialOrd for Version {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> {
        Some(self.cmp(other))
    }
}

fn valid_decimal(part: &str) -> bool {
    !part.is_empty()
        && part.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit())
        && (part == "0" || !part.starts_with('0'))
}

fn compare_decimal(left: &str, right: &str) -> Ordering {
    left.len().cmp(&right.len()).then_with(|| left.cmp(right))
}

fn increment_decimal(value: &str) -> String {
    let mut digits = value.as_bytes().to_vec();
    for digit in digits.iter_mut().rev() {
        if *digit < b'9' {
            *digit += 1;
            return String::from_utf8(digits).expect("ASCII decimal digits remain UTF-8");
        }
        *digit = b'0';
    }
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(value.len() + 1);
    result.push('1');
    result.push_str(std::str::from_utf8(&digits).expect("ASCII decimal digits remain UTF-8"));
    result
}

use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    env,
    ffi::{OsStr, OsString},
    fs,
    os::unix::{fs::PermissionsExt, process::CommandExt},
    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
    process::Command,
    time::Duration,
};

use crate::{
    filesystem::file_path,
    model::Client,
    process::{
        CancellationToken, CommandSpec, ProcessRunner, StdinMode, StreamMode, SystemProcessRunner,
    },
};

const PROJECT_METADATA: &str = "AI_PROFILE_PROJECT_DIR";
const COMPOSE_FILE: &str = "COMPOSE_FILE";
const BASIC_AUTH_FILE: &str = "AI_PROFILE_PLAYWRIGHT_BASIC_AUTH_FILE";
const STORAGE_STATE: &str = "AI_PROFILE_PLAYWRIGHT_STORAGE_STATE";
const DEFAULT_BASIC_AUTH: &str = "base-auth.json";
const CA_CERT: &str = "AI_PROFILE_PLAYWRIGHT_CA_CERT";
const CAROOT_OUTPUT_LIMIT: usize = 8192;

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub(crate) enum PlaywrightError {
    #[error("Invalid Playwright launcher location")]
    InvalidLauncher,
    #[error("Playwright project metadata does not match the installed launcher")]
    ProjectMismatch,
    #[error("Cannot prepare the Playwright reports directory")]
    InvalidReports,
    #[error("Invalid private Playwright input; check its path, file type and permissions")]
    InvalidPrivateInput,
    #[error("Cannot prepare Playwright Compose configuration")]
    InvalidCompose,
    #[error(
        "Invalid Playwright CA certificate path; expected an existing public certificate file"
    )]
    InvalidCa,
}

pub(crate) struct LaunchPlan {
    pub(crate) project: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) environment: HashMap<OsString, OsString>,
}

pub(crate) fn prepare_launch(
    executable: &Path,
    project_dir: &OsStr,
    inherited: &HashMap<OsString, OsString>,
) -> Result<LaunchPlan, PlaywrightError> {
    prepare_launch_with(executable, project_dir, inherited, &SystemProcessRunner)
}

pub(crate) fn prepare_launch_with(
    executable: &Path,
    project_dir: &OsStr,
    inherited: &HashMap<OsString, OsString>,
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
) -> Result<LaunchPlan, PlaywrightError> {
    let (project, launcher_directory) = installed_identity(executable)?;
    let metadata_project = fs::canonicalize(PathBuf::from(project_dir))
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::ProjectMismatch)?;
    if metadata_project != project {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::ProjectMismatch);
    }

    prepare_reports(&project)?;
    let reports = project
        .join(".ai-profiles/reports")
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidReports)?;

    let mut environment = HashMap::new();
    let mut overlays = Vec::new();
    if inherited
        .get(OsStr::new("AI_PROFILE_PLAYWRIGHT_NETWORK"))
        .is_none_or(|network| network.is_empty() || network == "host")
    {
        overlays.push(launcher_directory.join("compose.host.yaml"));
    }
    if let Some(certificate) = ca_certificate(&project, inherited, runner)? {
        environment.insert(OsString::from(CA_CERT), certificate.into_os_string());
        overlays.push(launcher_directory.join("compose.ca.yaml"));
    }
    if let Some(value) = inherited
        .get(OsStr::new(STORAGE_STATE))
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
    {
        let storage = private_input(&project, value, &reports)?;
        environment.insert(OsString::from(STORAGE_STATE), storage.into_os_string());
        overlays.push(launcher_directory.join("compose.auth.yaml"));
    }

    let explicit_auth = inherited
        .get(OsStr::new(BASIC_AUTH_FILE))
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty());
    let credentials = match explicit_auth {
        Some(value) => Some(private_input(&project, value, &reports)?),
        None => default_credentials(&project, &reports)?,
    };
    if let Some(credentials) = credentials {
        environment.insert(
            OsString::from(BASIC_AUTH_FILE),
            credentials.into_os_string(),
        );
        overlays.push(launcher_directory.join("compose.basic-auth.yaml"));
    }

    let original = inherited
        .get(OsStr::new(COMPOSE_FILE))
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        .map(|value| value.to_str().ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidCompose))
        .transpose()?;
    let compose_files = compose_files(
        &project,
        &launcher_directory.join("compose.yaml"),
        original,
        &overlays,
    )?;
    environment.insert(OsString::from(COMPOSE_FILE), OsString::from(compose_files));

    Ok(LaunchPlan {
        project,
        environment,
    })
}

fn ca_certificate(
    project: &Path,
    inherited: &HashMap<OsString, OsString>,
    runner: &impl ProcessRunner,
) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, PlaywrightError> {
    if let Some(explicit) = inherited
        .get(OsStr::new(CA_CERT))
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
    {
        return public_certificate(&absolute_path(project, Path::new(explicit))).map(Some);
    }
    if let Some(caroot) = inherited
        .get(OsStr::new("CAROOT"))
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
    {
        return existing_ca(&absolute_path(project, Path::new(caroot)));
    }
    let Some(program) = inherited.get(OsStr::new("PATH")).and_then(|path| {
        env::split_paths(path)
            .map(|directory| absolute_path(project, &directory).join("mkcert"))
            .find(|program| {
                fs::metadata(program).is_ok_and(|metadata| {
                    metadata.is_file() && metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o111 != 0
                })
            })
    }) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    // mkcert's CAROOT query only needs location-related environment, not MCP secrets.
    let environment = ["PATH", "HOME", "XDG_DATA_HOME"]
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|key| {
            inherited
                .get(OsStr::new(key))
                .map(|value| (OsString::from(key), value.clone()))
        })
        .collect();
    let output = runner.run(CommandSpec {
        program: program.into_os_string(),
        args: vec![OsString::from("-CAROOT")],
        cwd: Some(project.to_owned()),
        env: environment,
        clear_env: true,
        stdin: StdinMode::Null,
        stdout: StreamMode::CaptureLimited(CAROOT_OUTPUT_LIMIT),
        stderr: StreamMode::Null,
        timeout: Some(Duration::from_secs(5)),
        cancellation: CancellationToken::new(),
    });
    let caroot = output
        .ok()
        .filter(|output| output.status.success())
        .and_then(|output| parse_caroot(&output.stdout));
    match caroot {
        Some(caroot) => existing_ca(&caroot),
        None => {
            eprintln!("playwright: cannot discover mkcert CA; continuing without automatic CA");
            Ok(None)
        }
    }
}

fn absolute_path(project: &Path, path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    if path.is_absolute() {
        path.to_owned()
    } else {
        project.join(path)
    }
}

fn parse_caroot(output: &[u8]) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if output.len() > CAROOT_OUTPUT_LIMIT {
        return None;
    }
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(output).ok()?;
    let text = text.strip_suffix('\n').unwrap_or(text);
    let text = text.strip_suffix('\r').unwrap_or(text);
    let path = Path::new(text);
    (path.is_absolute() && !text.contains(['\n', '\r', '\0'])).then(|| path.to_owned())
}

fn existing_ca(caroot: &Path) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, PlaywrightError> {
    let path = caroot.join("rootCA.pem");
    match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(_) => Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidCa),
        Ok(_) => public_certificate(&path).map(Some),
    }
}

fn public_certificate(path: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, PlaywrightError> {
    // Only inspect metadata on the host. Certificate parsing/import stays in Docker.
    // Preserve explicit public-CA symlinks, but mount only the resolved file.
    let path = path
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidCa)?;
    if path.file_name() == Some(OsStr::new("rootCA-key.pem")) {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidCa);
    }
    let metadata = fs::metadata(&path).map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidCa)?;
    if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o444 == 0 {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidCa);
    }
    Ok(path)
}

fn installed_identity(executable: &Path) -> Result<(PathBuf, PathBuf), PlaywrightError> {
    let executable = executable
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    if executable.file_name() != Some(OsStr::new("playwright-launcher")) {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher);
    }
    let launcher_directory = executable
        .parent()
        .filter(|path| path.file_name() == Some(OsStr::new("playwright")))
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let mcp_directory = launcher_directory
        .parent()
        .filter(|path| path.file_name() == Some(OsStr::new("mcp")))
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let client_directory = mcp_directory
        .parent()
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let client_name = client_directory
        .file_name()
        .and_then(OsStr::to_str)
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    client_name
        .parse::<Client>()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let profiles_directory = client_directory
        .parent()
        .filter(|path| path.file_name() == Some(OsStr::new(".ai-profiles")))
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let project = profiles_directory
        .parent()
        .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    let launcher_directory = launcher_directory
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidLauncher)?;
    Ok((project, launcher_directory))
}

fn prepare_reports(project: &Path) -> Result<(), PlaywrightError> {
    let path =
        file_path(project, ".ai-profiles/reports").map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidReports)?;
    match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
        Ok(metadata) if metadata.is_dir() && !metadata.file_type().is_symlink() => Ok(()),
        Ok(_) => Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidReports),
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => match fs::create_dir(&path) {
            Ok(()) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {
                fs::symlink_metadata(&path)
                    .ok()
                    .filter(|metadata| metadata.is_dir() && !metadata.file_type().is_symlink())
                    .map(|_| ())
                    .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidReports)
            }
            Err(_) => Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidReports),
        },
        Err(_) => Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidReports),
    }
}

fn default_credentials(project: &Path, reports: &Path) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, PlaywrightError> {
    let path =
        file_path(project, DEFAULT_BASIC_AUTH).map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput)?;
    match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(_) => Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput),
        Ok(_) => private_input(project, OsStr::new(DEFAULT_BASIC_AUTH), reports).map(Some),
    }
}

fn private_input(
    project: &Path,
    value: &OsStr,
    reports: &Path,
) -> Result<PathBuf, PlaywrightError> {
    let supplied = PathBuf::from(value);
    let path = if supplied.is_absolute() {
        if has_parent_component(&supplied) || has_symlink_component(&supplied) {
            return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput);
        }
        supplied
    } else {
        let relative = supplied
            .to_str()
            .ok_or(PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput)?;
        file_path(project, relative).map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput)?
    };
    let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput)?;
    if !metadata.is_file()
        || metadata.file_type().is_symlink()
        || metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o400 == 0
        || metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o077 != 0
    {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput);
    }
    let canonical = path
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|_| PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput)?;
    if canonical.starts_with(reports) || !canonical.starts_with(project) {
        return Err(PlaywrightError::InvalidPrivateInput);
    }
    Ok(canonical)
}

fn has_parent_component(path: &Path) -> bool {
    path.components()
        .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::ParentDir))
}

fn has_symlink_component(path: &Path) -> bool {
    let mut current = PathBuf::new();
    for component in path.components() {
        current.push(component.as_os_str());
        if fs::symlink_metadata(&current).is_ok_and(|metadata| metadata.file_type().is_symlink()) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}

fn compose_files(
    project: &Path,
    base: &Path,
    original: Option<&str>,
    overlays: &[PathBuf],
) -> Result<String, PlaywrightError> {
    let original_files = original
        .map(|value| value.split(':').map(str::to_owned).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        .filter(|files| !files.is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| vec![base.to_string_lossy().into_owned()]);
    let mut combined = Vec::with_capacity(overlays.len() + original_files.len());
    for overlay in overlays {
        if !original_files
            .iter()
            .any(|entry| same_compose_path(project, entry, overlay))
        {
            combined.push(overlay.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        }
    }
    combined.extend(original_files);
    Ok(combined.join(":"))
}

fn same_compose_path(project: &Path, entry: &str, expected: &Path) -> bool {
    let entry = Path::new(entry);
    let resolved = if entry.is_absolute() {
        entry.to_owned()
    } else {
        project.join(entry)
    };
    match (resolved.canonicalize(), expected.canonicalize()) {
        (Ok(resolved), Ok(expected)) => resolved == expected,
        _ => resolved == expected,
    }
}

pub fn main() -> i32 {
    let mut arguments = env::args_os();
    let _executable = arguments.next();
    if arguments.next().is_some() {
        eprintln!("playwright: launcher does not accept arguments");
        return 2;
    }
    let executable = match env::current_exe() {
        Ok(path) => path,
        Err(_) => {
            eprintln!("playwright: invalid installed launcher location");
            return 1;
        }
    };
    let Some(project) = env::var_os(PROJECT_METADATA) else {
        eprintln!("playwright: start through the installed ai-profile-mcp adapter");
        return 1;
    };
    let inherited = env::vars_os().collect::<HashMap<_, _>>();
    let plan = match prepare_launch(&executable, &project, &inherited) {
        Ok(plan) => plan,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("playwright: {error}");
            return 1;
        }
    };

    let mut command = Command::new("docker");
    command
        .arg("compose")
        .arg("--project-directory")
        .arg(&plan.project)
        .args([
            "run",
            "--rm",
            "--no-deps",
            "--pull",
            "never",
            "-T",
            "playwright",
        ])
        .current_dir(&plan.project)
        .envs(plan.environment);
    let source = command.exec();
    eprintln!("playwright: cannot start Docker Compose: {source}");
    1
}

use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    ffi::OsString,
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::PathBuf,
    process::{Child, Command, ExitStatus, Stdio},
    sync::{
        atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering},
        Arc,
    },
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

use crate::error::ProcessError;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum StreamMode {
    Capture,
    // Keep at most limit + 1 bytes, allowing the caller to reject overflow.
    CaptureLimited(usize),
    Inherit,
    Null,
    RedirectToStderr,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
#[allow(dead_code)] // The process boundary supports callers that inherit or pipe stdin.
pub(crate) enum StdinMode {
    Bytes(Vec<u8>),
    Inherit,
    Null,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct CancellationToken(Arc<AtomicBool>);

impl CancellationToken {
    pub(crate) fn new() -> Self {
        Self(Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn cancel(&self) {
        self.0.store(true, Ordering::Release);
    }

    pub(crate) fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct CommandSpec {
    pub(crate) program: OsString,
    pub(crate) args: Vec<OsString>,
    pub(crate) cwd: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub(crate) env: HashMap<OsString, OsString>,
    pub(crate) clear_env: bool,
    pub(crate) stdin: StdinMode,
    pub(crate) stdout: StreamMode,
    pub(crate) stderr: StreamMode,
    pub(crate) timeout: Option<Duration>,
    pub(crate) cancellation: CancellationToken,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct CommandOutput {
    pub(crate) status: ExitStatus,
    pub(crate) stdout: Vec<u8>,
    pub(crate) stderr: Vec<u8>,
}

pub(crate) trait ProcessRunner {
    fn run(&self, spec: CommandSpec) -> Result<CommandOutput, ProcessError>;
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub(crate) struct SystemProcessRunner;

impl ProcessRunner for SystemProcessRunner {
    fn run(&self, spec: CommandSpec) -> Result<CommandOutput, ProcessError> {
        if spec.cancellation.is_cancelled() {
            return Err(ProcessError::Cancelled);
        }

        let mut command = Command::new(&spec.program);
        command.args(&spec.args);
        if let Some(cwd) = &spec.cwd {
            command.current_dir(cwd);
        }
        if spec.clear_env {
            command.env_clear();
        }
        command.envs(&spec.env);
        command.stdin(stdin_stdio(&spec.stdin));
        command.stdout(stream_stdio(spec.stdout, true));
        command.stderr(stream_stdio(spec.stderr, false));

        let mut child = command
            .spawn()
            .map_err(|source| ProcessError::Spawn { source })?;
        let stdin_writer = take_stdin(&mut child, &spec.stdin);
        let stdout_reader = take_reader(child.stdout.take(), spec.stdout);
        let stderr_reader = take_reader(child.stderr.take(), spec.stderr);
        let started = Instant::now();

        let result = wait_for_child(&mut child, started, spec.timeout, &spec.cancellation);
        let status = match result {
            Ok(status) => status,
            Err(error) => {
                let _ = child.kill();
                let _ = child.wait();
                // A descendant may still hold an inherited pipe open. Do not
                // defeat the process deadline by joining its stream readers.
                return Err(error);
            }
        };
        let writer_result = join_writer(stdin_writer);
        let stdout_result = join_reader(stdout_reader, spec.stdout, started, spec.timeout);
        let stderr_result = join_reader(stderr_reader, spec.stderr, started, spec.timeout);
        writer_result?;
        let stdout = stdout_result?;
        let stderr = stderr_result?;

        Ok(CommandOutput {
            status,
            stdout,
            stderr,
        })
    }
}

fn stdin_stdio(mode: &StdinMode) -> Stdio {
    match mode {
        StdinMode::Bytes(_) => Stdio::piped(),
        StdinMode::Inherit => Stdio::inherit(),
        StdinMode::Null => Stdio::null(),
    }
}

fn stream_stdio(mode: StreamMode, is_stdout: bool) -> Stdio {
    match mode {
        StreamMode::Capture | StreamMode::CaptureLimited(_) => Stdio::piped(),
        StreamMode::Inherit => Stdio::inherit(),
        StreamMode::Null => Stdio::null(),
        StreamMode::RedirectToStderr if is_stdout => Stdio::from(std::io::stderr()),
        StreamMode::RedirectToStderr => Stdio::inherit(),
    }
}

fn take_stdin(
    child: &mut Child,
    mode: &StdinMode,
) -> Option<thread::JoinHandle<std::io::Result<()>>> {
    let StdinMode::Bytes(bytes) = mode else {
        return None;
    };
    let mut stdin = child.stdin.take()?;
    let bytes = bytes.clone();
    Some(thread::spawn(move || stdin.write_all(&bytes)))
}

fn take_reader<R: Read + Send + 'static>(
    reader: Option<R>,
    mode: StreamMode,
) -> Option<thread::JoinHandle<std::io::Result<Vec<u8>>>> {
    reader.map(|mut reader| {
        thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut bytes = Vec::new();
            if let StreamMode::CaptureLimited(limit) = mode {
                reader
                    .take((limit as u64).saturating_add(1))
                    .read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
            } else {
                reader.read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
            }
            Ok(bytes)
        })
    })
}

fn join_writer(
    writer: Option<thread::JoinHandle<std::io::Result<()>>>,
) -> Result<(), ProcessError> {
    if let Some(writer) = writer {
        writer
            .join()
            .map_err(|_| ProcessError::StreamThread)?
            .map_err(|source| ProcessError::Io { source })?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn join_reader(
    reader: Option<thread::JoinHandle<std::io::Result<Vec<u8>>>>,
    mode: StreamMode,
    started: Instant,
    timeout: Option<Duration>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, ProcessError> {
    let Some(reader) = reader else {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    };
    if let (StreamMode::CaptureLimited(_), Some(limit)) = (mode, timeout) {
        while !reader.is_finished() {
            if started.elapsed() >= limit {
                return Err(ProcessError::Timeout);
            }
            thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
        }
    }
    reader
        .join()
        .map_err(|_| ProcessError::StreamThread)?
        .map_err(|source| ProcessError::Io { source })
}

fn wait_for_child(
    child: &mut Child,
    started: Instant,
    timeout: Option<Duration>,
    cancellation: &CancellationToken,
) -> Result<ExitStatus, ProcessError> {
    loop {
        if cancellation.is_cancelled() {
            return Err(ProcessError::Cancelled);
        }
        if let Some(status) = child
            .try_wait()
            .map_err(|source| ProcessError::Io { source })?
        {
            return Ok(status);
        }
        if timeout.is_some_and(|limit| started.elapsed() >= limit) {
            return Err(ProcessError::Timeout);
        }
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
    }
}

use std::os::unix::ffi::{OsStrExt, OsStringExt};
use std::{
    env,
    ffi::{OsStr, OsString},
    path::PathBuf,
};

use crate::{
    model::{ClientSelection, ProfileAction, RepositoryContext, VersionBump},
    profile_management::{
        migration::migrate_legacy,
        profiles::{bump_profile, create_profile, rename_profile},
        validation::validate_sources,
    },
    project::selected_project,
    repository::profile_names,
};

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "../../tests/cli.rs"]
mod cli_tests;

const COMMAND_HELP: &str = include_str!("../../help.txt");

struct Options {
    client: Option<ClientSelection>,
    action: Option<ProfileAction>,
    profile: Option<String>,
    project: Option<PathBuf>,
    accept_existing: bool,
    part: Option<VersionBump>,
    description: Option<String>,
    new_name: Option<String>,
}

enum CliFailure {
    Parse(String),
    Operation(String),
}

pub fn main() -> i32 {
    let mut args = env::args_os();
    let _program = args.next();
    let parsed = match parse_args(args.collect()) {
        Ok(ParseResult::Help) => {
            print!("{COMMAND_HELP}");
            return 0;
        }
        Ok(ParseResult::Options(options)) => options,
        Err(message) => return parser_error(&message),
    };

    if let Err(message) = validate_option_combinations(&parsed) {
        return parser_error(&message);
    }
    if matches!(parsed.action, None | Some(ProfileAction::Help)) {
        print!("{COMMAND_HELP}");
        return 0;
    }
    match run(parsed) {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(CliFailure::Parse(message)) => parser_error(&message),
        Err(CliFailure::Operation(message)) => {
            eprintln!("ERROR: {message}");
            1
        }
    }
}

enum ParseResult {
    Help,
    Options(Options),
}

fn parse_args(args: Vec<OsString>) -> Result<ParseResult, String> {
    let mut action = None;
    let mut client = None;
    let mut profile = None;
    let mut positional_count = 0;
    let mut project = None;
    let mut accept_existing = false;
    let mut part = None;
    let mut description = None;
    let mut new_name = None;
    let mut positional_only = false;
    let mut index = 0;
    while index < args.len() {
        let argument = &args[index];
        if !positional_only && argument == OsStr::new("--project") {
            let value = option_os_value(&args, &mut index, "--project")?;
            project = Some(if value.is_empty() {
                PathBuf::from(".")
            } else {
                PathBuf::from(value)
            });
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only {
            if let Some(value) = attached_os_value(argument, b"--project=") {
                project = Some(if value.is_empty() {
                    PathBuf::from(".")
                } else {
                    PathBuf::from(value)
                });
                index += 1;
                continue;
            }
        }
        let Some(text) = argument.to_str() else {
            return Err("argument must be valid UTF-8".to_owned());
        };
        if !positional_only && matches!(text, "-h" | "--help") {
            return Ok(ParseResult::Help);
        }
        if !positional_only && text == "--" {
            positional_only = true;
            index += 1;
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && text == "--accept-existing" {
            accept_existing = true;
            index += 1;
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && (text == "--part" || text.starts_with("--part=")) {
            let value = option_value(&args, &mut index, "--part")?;
            part = Some(match value.as_str() {
                "major" => VersionBump::Major,
                "minor" => VersionBump::Minor,
                "patch" => VersionBump::Patch,
                _ => return Err(format!("argument --part: invalid choice: '{value}' (choose from 'major', 'minor', 'patch')")),
            });
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && (text == "--description" || text.starts_with("--description=")) {
            description = Some(option_value(&args, &mut index, "--description")?);
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && (text == "--client" || text.starts_with("--client=")) {
            if client.is_some() {
                return Err("--client podano więcej niż raz".into());
            }
            client = Some(ClientSelection::parse(&option_value(
                &args, &mut index, "--client",
            )?)?);
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && (text == "--new-name" || text.starts_with("--new-name=")) {
            new_name = Some(option_value(&args, &mut index, "--new-name")?);
            continue;
        }
        if !positional_only && text.starts_with('-') {
            return Err(format!("unrecognized arguments: {text}"));
        }
        match positional_count {
            0 => action = Some(parse_action(text)?),
            1 => profile = Some(text.to_owned()),
            _ => return Err(format!("unrecognized arguments: {text}")),
        }
        positional_count += 1;
        index += 1;
    }

    Ok(ParseResult::Options(Options {
        client,
        action,
        profile,
        project,
        accept_existing,
        part,
        description,
        new_name,
    }))
}

fn option_value(args: &[OsString], index: &mut usize, option: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let text = args[*index]
        .to_str()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("argument {option}: expected a UTF-8 value"))?;
    if let Some((_, value)) = text.split_once('=') {
        *index += 1;
        return Ok(value.to_owned());
    }
    let next = args
        .get(*index + 1)
        .and_then(|value| value.to_str())
        .filter(|value| !value.starts_with('-'))
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("argument {option}: expected one argument"))?;
    *index += 2;
    Ok(next.to_owned())
}

fn option_os_value(args: &[OsString], index: &mut usize, option: &str) -> Result<OsString, String> {
    let next = args
        .get(*index + 1)
        .filter(|value| !value.as_bytes().starts_with(b"-"))
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("argument {option}: expected one argument"))?;
    *index += 2;
    Ok(next.clone())
}

fn attached_os_value(argument: &OsStr, prefix: &[u8]) -> Option<OsString> {
    argument
        .as_bytes()
        .strip_prefix(prefix)
        .map(|value| OsString::from_vec(value.to_vec()))
}

fn parse_action(value: &str) -> Result<ProfileAction, String> {
    match value {
        "create" => Ok(ProfileAction::Create),
        "rename" => Ok(ProfileAction::Rename),
        "help" => Ok(ProfileAction::Help),
        "install" => Ok(ProfileAction::Install),
        "update" => Ok(ProfileAction::Update),
        "uninstall" => Ok(ProfileAction::Uninstall),
        "switch" => Ok(ProfileAction::Switch),
        "status" => Ok(ProfileAction::Status),
        "validate" => Ok(ProfileAction::Validate),
        "migrate-legacy" => Ok(ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy),
        "bump" => Ok(ProfileAction::Bump),
        _ => Err(format!("argument action: invalid choice: '{value}'")),
    }
}

fn validate_option_combinations(options: &Options) -> Result<(), String> {
    let action = options.action;
    let lifecycle = matches!(
        action,
        Some(
            ProfileAction::Install
                | ProfileAction::Update
                | ProfileAction::Switch
                | ProfileAction::Uninstall
        )
    );
    if lifecycle && options.client.is_none() {
        return Err("Wymagane --client opencode|claude|codex|all".into());
    }
    if options.client.is_some() && !lifecycle && action != Some(ProfileAction::Status) {
        return Err(
            "--client jest dostępne tylko dla install, update, switch, uninstall i status".into(),
        );
    }
    if options.new_name.is_some() && action != Some(ProfileAction::Rename) {
        return Err("--new-name jest dostępne tylko dla rename".to_owned());
    }
    if action == Some(ProfileAction::Rename)
        && options.new_name.as_deref().is_none_or(str::is_empty)
    {
        return Err("rename wymaga --new-name NOWA_NAZWA".to_owned());
    }
    if options.part.is_some() && action != Some(ProfileAction::Bump) {
        return Err("--part jest dostępne tylko dla bump".to_owned());
    }
    if options.description.is_some() && action != Some(ProfileAction::Create) {
        return Err("--description jest dostępne tylko dla create".to_owned());
    }
    if options.profile.is_some()
        && !matches!(
            action,
            Some(
                ProfileAction::Create
                    | ProfileAction::Rename
                    | ProfileAction::Install
                    | ProfileAction::Switch
                    | ProfileAction::Bump
                    | ProfileAction::Validate
            )
        )
    {
        return Err("Ta komenda nie przyjmuje nazwy profilu".to_owned());
    }
    if options.project.is_some()
        && matches!(
            action,
            None | Some(
                ProfileAction::Help
                    | ProfileAction::Create
                    | ProfileAction::Rename
                    | ProfileAction::Bump
                    | ProfileAction::Validate
            )
        )
    {
        return Err("Ta komenda nie używa --project".to_owned());
    }
    if options.accept_existing && action != Some(ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy) {
        return Err("--accept-existing jest dostępne tylko dla migrate-legacy".to_owned());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn run(options: Options) -> Result<(), CliFailure> {
    if matches!(
        options.action,
        Some(
            ProfileAction::Create
                | ProfileAction::Rename
                | ProfileAction::Install
                | ProfileAction::Switch
                | ProfileAction::Bump
        )
    ) && options.profile.as_deref().is_none_or(str::is_empty)
    {
        let Some(action) = options.action else {
            return Err(CliFailure::Parse("brak komendy".to_owned()));
        };
        let action = action_name(action);
        return Err(CliFailure::Parse(format!("{action} wymaga nazwy profilu")));
    }

    let context = RepositoryContext::discover().map_err(operation_error)?;
    if matches!(
        options.action,
        Some(
            ProfileAction::Install
                | ProfileAction::Switch
                | ProfileAction::Bump
                | ProfileAction::Rename
        )
    ) {
        let profile = options.profile.as_deref().ok_or_else(|| {
            CliFailure::Parse("install/switch/bump/rename wymagają nazwy profilu".to_owned())
        })?;
        if !profile_names(&context)
            .map_err(operation_error)?
            .iter()
            .any(|name| name == profile)
        {
            return Err(CliFailure::Parse(format!("Nieznany profil: {profile}")));
        }
    }

    match options.action {
        Some(ProfileAction::Rename) => rename_profile(
            &context,
            options
                .profile
                .as_deref()
                .ok_or_else(|| CliFailure::Parse("rename wymaga nazwy profilu".to_owned()))?,
            options
                .new_name
                .as_deref()
                .ok_or_else(|| CliFailure::Parse("rename wymaga --new-name".to_owned()))?,
        )
        .map_err(operation_error),
        Some(ProfileAction::Create) => create_profile(
            &context,
            options
                .profile
                .as_deref()
                .ok_or_else(|| CliFailure::Parse("create wymaga nazwy profilu".to_owned()))?,
            options.description.as_deref(),
        )
        .map_err(operation_error),
        Some(ProfileAction::Bump) => {
            let profile = options.profile.as_deref().ok_or_else(|| {
                CliFailure::Parse("bump wymaga nazwy profilu źródłowego".to_owned())
            })?;
            bump_profile(
                &context,
                profile,
                options.part.unwrap_or(VersionBump::Patch),
            )
            .map(|_| ())
            .map_err(operation_error)
        }
        Some(ProfileAction::Validate) => {
            validate_sources(&context, options.profile.as_deref()).map_err(operation_error)
        }
        Some(
            ProfileAction::Install
            | ProfileAction::Switch
            | ProfileAction::Update
            | ProfileAction::Uninstall
            | ProfileAction::Status
            | ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy,
        ) => {
            let project =
                selected_project(&context, options.project.as_deref()).map_err(operation_error)?;
            if let Some(
                action @ (ProfileAction::Install
                | ProfileAction::Update
                | ProfileAction::Switch
                | ProfileAction::Uninstall
                | ProfileAction::Status),
            ) = options.action
            {
                return crate::profile_management::scoped::run(
                    &context,
                    &project,
                    action,
                    options.client.unwrap_or(ClientSelection::All),
                    options.profile.as_deref(),
                )
                .map_err(operation_error);
            }
            match options.action {
                Some(ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy) => {
                    migrate_legacy(&context, &project, options.accept_existing)
                        .map_err(operation_error)
                }
                _ => Err(CliFailure::Operation("Nieobsługiwana komenda".to_owned())),
            }
        }
        None | Some(ProfileAction::Help) => Ok(()),
    }
}

fn action_name(action: ProfileAction) -> &'static str {
    match action {
        ProfileAction::Help => "help",
        ProfileAction::Create => "create",
        ProfileAction::Rename => "rename",
        ProfileAction::Install => "install",
        ProfileAction::Update => "update",
        ProfileAction::Uninstall => "uninstall",
        ProfileAction::Switch => "switch",
        ProfileAction::Status => "status",
        ProfileAction::Validate => "validate",
        ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy => "migrate-legacy",
        ProfileAction::Bump => "bump",
    }
}

fn operation_error(error: impl std::fmt::Display) -> CliFailure {
    CliFailure::Operation(error.to_string())
}

fn parser_error(message: &str) -> i32 {
    eprintln!("usage: ai-profile KOMENDA [NAZWA] [opcje] (pomoc: make help)");
    eprintln!("ai-profile: error: {message}");
    2
}
