use std::{
    collections::HashSet,
    ffi::OsStr,
    fs,
    os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
};

use regex::Regex;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::OnceLock;

use crate::{
    error::PayloadError, filesystem::file_path, mcp::config::desired_mcp, model::RepositoryContext,
};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct SkillName(String);

impl SkillName {
    fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, ()> {
        static NAME: OnceLock<Regex> = OnceLock::new();
        let valid = NAME.get_or_init(|| {
            Regex::new(r"^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$").expect("fixed skill-name regex is valid")
        });
        if valid.is_match(value) {
            Ok(Self(value.to_owned()))
        } else {
            Err(())
        }
    }
    fn as_str(&self) -> &str {
        &self.0
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum PayloadKind {
    Data,
    Executable,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) struct PayloadFile {
    pub(crate) relative_path: String,
    pub(crate) contents: Vec<u8>,
    pub(crate) kind: PayloadKind,
}

pub(crate) fn desired_files(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<PayloadFile>, PayloadError> {
    build_files(context, profile, None)
}

pub(crate) fn desired_files_for(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
    client: crate::model::Client,
) -> Result<Vec<PayloadFile>, PayloadError> {
    build_files(context, profile, Some(client))
}

fn build_files(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
    client: Option<crate::model::Client>,
) -> Result<Vec<PayloadFile>, PayloadError> {
    use crate::model::Client;
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    let selected = selected_skills(context, profile)?;
    for name in selected {
        let folder = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("skills/{}", name.as_str()))?;
        collect_skill(context, &name, &folder, &mut files)?;
    }

    // Only the selected native client receives a manifest. None is used for
    // source validation and historical combined-installation test fixtures.
    let metadata = crate::repository::profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let description = metadata
        .get("description")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("");
    let version = metadata
        .get("version")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("1.0.0");
    let manifest = serde_json::json!({"name":"ai-profile","version":version,"description":description,"skills":["./skills/"]});
    let manifest_bytes = crate::codec::json_bytes(&manifest)?;
    if client.is_none_or(|c| c == Client::Claude) {
        files.push(PayloadFile {
            relative_path: ".opencode/ai-profile/plugin/.claude-plugin/plugin.json".into(),
            contents: manifest_bytes.clone(),
            kind: PayloadKind::Data,
        });
    }
    if client.is_none_or(|c| c == Client::Codex) {
        files.push(PayloadFile {
            relative_path: ".opencode/ai-profile/plugin/.codex-plugin/plugin.json".into(),
            contents: manifest_bytes,
            kind: PayloadKind::Data,
        });
    }
    // A real empty directory is ensured by installation when selection is empty.

    let definitions = desired_mcp(context, profile)?;
    if client.is_none()
        || definitions
            .iter()
            .any(|definition| definition.command.is_some())
    {
        insert_native(
            context,
            &mut files,
            "ai-profile-mcp",
            ".opencode/ai-profile-mcp",
        )?;
    }

    for definition in definitions {
        let name = definition.name.as_str();
        if name == "mariadb" {
            insert_native(
                context,
                &mut files,
                "mariadb-launcher",
                ".opencode/mcp/mariadb/mariadb-launcher",
            )?;
        }
        if name == "playwright" {
            insert_native(
                context,
                &mut files,
                "playwright-launcher",
                ".opencode/mcp/playwright/playwright-launcher",
            )?;
            // Distribute a self-contained build context and standard Docker config.
            for filename in [
                "Dockerfile",
                "entrypoint.sh",
                "basic-auth.cjs",
                "LICENSES.md",
                "README.md",
                ".dockerignore",
                "compose.yaml",
                "compose.host.yaml",
                "compose.ca.yaml",
                "compose.auth.yaml",
                "compose.basic-auth.yaml",
            ] {
                let source = file_path(
                    &context.sources,
                    &format!("mcp/images/playwright/{filename}"),
                )?;
                insert_file(
                    &mut files,
                    &format!(".opencode/mcp/playwright/{filename}"),
                    &source,
                    true,
                )?;
            }
        }
        if name == "novamira" {
            let root = file_path(&context.sources, "mcp/images/novamira")?;
            for (filename, destination) in [
                ("Dockerfile", "Dockerfile"),
                (".dockerignore", ".dockerignore"),
                ("compose.yaml", "compose.yaml"),
                ("compose.ca.yaml", "compose.ca.yaml"),
                ("README.md", "README.md"),
                ("novamira.zip", "downloads/novamira.zip"),
            ] {
                insert_file(
                    &mut files,
                    &format!(".opencode/mcp/novamira/{destination}"),
                    &root.join(filename),
                    true,
                )?;
            }
            for filename in ["launcher", "install-plugin"] {
                insert_executable_file(
                    &mut files,
                    &format!(".opencode/mcp/novamira/{filename}"),
                    &root.join(filename),
                )?;
            }
        }
        let launcher = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("mcp/launchers/{name}"))?;
        if launcher.exists() {
            if !launcher.is_dir() {
                return Err(PayloadError::InvalidLauncherDirectory(name.to_owned()));
            }
            for source in sorted_tree(&launcher)? {
                if source_is_symlink(&source)? {
                    return Err(PayloadError::LauncherSymlink(source));
                }
                let relative = source
                    .strip_prefix(&launcher)
                    .map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidPayloadPath(source.clone()))?;
                if contains_component(relative, "__pycache__") || has_extension(&source, "pyc") {
                    continue;
                }
                if name == "mariadb" && relative == Path::new("launch.py") {
                    continue; // Preserved reference; distributed as mariadb-launcher.
                }
                if source.is_file() {
                    let relative_path =
                        payload_path(&[".opencode/mcp", name, &path_to_posix(relative)?]);
                    insert_file(&mut files, &relative_path, &source, false)?;
                }
            }
        }

        for argument in definition.command.iter().flatten() {
            if argument.starts_with(".opencode/mcp/")
                && !files.iter().any(|file| file.relative_path == *argument)
            {
                return Err(PayloadError::MissingManagedLauncher(argument.clone()));
            }
        }
    }

    let readme = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("profiles/{profile}/README.md"))?;
    let scripts = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("profiles/{profile}/scripts"))?;
    if scripts.join("restore-updraft.py").is_file() {
        insert_native(
            context,
            &mut files,
            "restore-updraft",
            ".opencode/ai-profile/scripts/restore-updraft",
        )?;
    }
    if scripts.exists() {
        if !scripts.is_dir() {
            return Err(PayloadError::InvalidScriptsDirectory(profile.to_owned()));
        }
        for source in sorted_tree(&scripts)? {
            if source_is_symlink(&source)? {
                return Err(PayloadError::ScriptSymlink(source));
            }
            let relative = source
                .strip_prefix(&scripts)
                .map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidPayloadPath(source.clone()))?;
            if contains_component(relative, "__pycache__") || has_extension(&source, "pyc") {
                continue;
            }
            if relative == Path::new("restore-updraft.py") {
                continue; // Preserved reference; distributed as restore-updraft.
            }
            if source.is_file() {
                let relative_path =
                    payload_path(&[".opencode/ai-profile/scripts", &path_to_posix(relative)?]);
                insert_file(&mut files, &relative_path, &source, false)?;
            }
        }
    }
    if readme.is_file() {
        insert_file(&mut files, ".opencode/ai-profile/README.md", &readme, false)?;
    }
    Ok(files)
}

fn selected_skills(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<SkillName>, PayloadError> {
    let relative = format!("profiles/{profile}/skills.json");
    let manifest_path = match file_path(&context.sources, &relative) {
        Ok(path) => path,
        Err(crate::error::FilesystemError::Symlink(path)) => {
            return Err(PayloadError::SelectedSkillSymlink {
                profile: profile.to_owned(),
                path,
            });
        }
        Err(error) => return Err(error.into()),
    };
    let bytes = fs::read(&manifest_path).map_err(|source| PayloadError::SkillManifestRead {
        profile: profile.to_owned(),
        path: manifest_path.clone(),
        source,
    })?;
    let manifest: Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidSkillManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
            path: manifest_path.clone(),
        })?;
    let Some(object) = manifest.as_object() else {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
            path: manifest_path,
        });
    };
    if object.len() != 1 {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
            path: manifest_path,
        });
    }
    let Some(entries) = object.get("skills").and_then(Value::as_array) else {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillManifest {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
            path: manifest_path,
        });
    };
    let mut seen = HashSet::with_capacity(entries.len());
    let mut names = Vec::with_capacity(entries.len());
    for entry in entries {
        let Some(raw) = entry.as_str() else {
            return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillManifest {
                profile: profile.to_owned(),
                path: manifest_path.clone(),
            });
        };
        let name = SkillName::parse(raw).map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidSelectedSkill {
            profile: profile.to_owned(),
            path: manifest_path.clone(),
            name: raw.to_owned(),
        })?;
        if !seen.insert(name.clone()) {
            return Err(PayloadError::DuplicateSelectedSkill {
                profile: profile.to_owned(),
                path: manifest_path.clone(),
                name: raw.to_owned(),
            });
        }
        let skill_relative = format!("skills/{}", name.as_str());
        let skill_dir = match file_path(&context.sources, &skill_relative) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(crate::error::FilesystemError::Symlink(path)) => {
                return Err(PayloadError::SelectedSkillSymlink {
                    profile: profile.to_owned(),
                    path,
                });
            }
            Err(error) => return Err(error.into()),
        };
        if !skill_dir.is_dir() {
            return Err(PayloadError::UnknownSelectedSkill {
                profile: profile.to_owned(),
                path: manifest_path.clone(),
                name: raw.to_owned(),
            });
        }
        names.push(name);
    }
    Ok(names)
}

pub(crate) fn validate_skill_catalog(context: &RepositoryContext) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    let root = file_path(&context.sources, "skills")?;
    let mut entries = read_directory(&root)?;
    entries.sort();
    for entry in entries {
        if entry.file_name().and_then(OsStr::to_str) == Some("README.md") {
            continue;
        }
        let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&entry).map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
            path: entry.clone(),
            source,
        })?;
        if !metadata.is_dir() || metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
            return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillDirectory(entry));
        }
        let raw = entry
            .file_name()
            .and_then(OsStr::to_str)
            .ok_or_else(|| PayloadError::InvalidPayloadPath(entry.clone()))?;
        let name = SkillName::parse(raw)
            .map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidSkillDirectory(entry.clone()))?;
        collect_skill(context, &name, &entry, &mut Vec::new())?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn collect_skill(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    name: &SkillName,
    folder: &Path,
    files: &mut Vec<PayloadFile>,
) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    let skill_name = name.as_str();
    let skill_file = folder.join("SKILL.md");
    let document_type = fs::symlink_metadata(&skill_file).ok();
    if !document_type
        .as_ref()
        .is_some_and(|metadata| metadata.is_file() && !metadata.file_type().is_symlink())
    {
        return Err(PayloadError::MissingSkillDocument(skill_file));
    }
    let frontmatter = fs::read_to_string(&skill_file).map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
        path: skill_file.clone(),
        source,
    })?;
    if !frontmatter.starts_with("---\n") {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidFrontmatter(skill_file));
    }
    let Some((metadata, _rest)) = frontmatter[4..].split_once("\n---") else {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidFrontmatter(skill_file));
    };
    let Some((declared_name, has_description)) = skill_metadata(metadata) else {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillMetadata {
            name: skill_name.to_owned(),
            path: skill_file,
        });
    };
    if declared_name != skill_name || !has_description {
        return Err(PayloadError::InvalidSkillMetadata {
            name: skill_name.to_owned(),
            path: skill_file,
        });
    }

    for source in tree_in_filesystem_order(folder)? {
        if source_is_symlink(&source)? {
            return Err(PayloadError::SkillSymlink(source));
        }
        if source.is_file() {
            let relative = source
                .strip_prefix(folder)
                .map_err(|_| PayloadError::InvalidPayloadPath(source.clone()))?;
            if skill_name == "test-gap-discovery"
                && relative == Path::new("scripts/inventory-tests.sh")
            {
                insert_native(
                    context,
                    files,
                    "test-inventory",
                    ".opencode/ai-profile/plugin/skills/test-gap-discovery/scripts/inventory-tests",
                )?;
                continue;
            }
            let relative_path = payload_path(&[
                ".opencode/ai-profile/plugin/skills",
                skill_name,
                &path_to_posix(relative)?,
            ]);
            insert_file(files, &relative_path, &source, true)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn skill_metadata(metadata: &str) -> Option<(&str, bool)> {
    static NAME: OnceLock<Regex> = OnceLock::new();
    static DESCRIPTION: OnceLock<Regex> = OnceLock::new();
    let name = NAME.get_or_init(|| {
        Regex::new(r#"(?m)^name:\s*["']?([a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*)["']?\s*$"#)
            .expect("fixed skill-name regex is valid")
    });
    let description = DESCRIPTION.get_or_init(|| {
        Regex::new(r"(?m)^description:\s*.+$").expect("fixed description regex is valid")
    });
    Some((
        name.captures(metadata)?.get(1)?.as_str(),
        description.is_match(metadata),
    ))
}

fn read_directory(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, PayloadError> {
    fs::read_dir(path)
        .map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
            path: path.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?
        .map(|entry| {
            entry
                .map(|entry| entry.path())
                .map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
                    path: path.to_owned(),
                    source,
                })
        })
        .collect()
}

fn tree_in_filesystem_order(root: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, PayloadError> {
    let mut entries = Vec::new();
    collect_tree(root, &mut entries)?;
    Ok(entries)
}

fn sorted_tree(root: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, PayloadError> {
    let mut entries = tree_in_filesystem_order(root)?;
    entries.sort();
    Ok(entries)
}

fn collect_tree(root: &Path, entries: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    for path in read_directory(root)? {
        entries.push(path.clone());
        let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
            path: path.clone(),
            source,
        })?;
        if metadata.is_dir() && !metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
            collect_tree(&path, entries)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn source_is_symlink(path: &Path) -> Result<bool, PayloadError> {
    fs::symlink_metadata(path)
        .map(|metadata| metadata.file_type().is_symlink())
        .map_err(|source| PayloadError::Io {
            path: path.to_owned(),
            source,
        })
}

fn has_extension(path: &Path, extension: &str) -> bool {
    path.extension() == Some(OsStr::new(extension))
}

fn contains_component(path: &Path, expected: &str) -> bool {
    path.components()
        .any(|component| component.as_os_str() == expected)
}

fn path_to_posix(path: &Path) -> Result<String, PayloadError> {
    path.components()
        .map(|component| {
            component
                .as_os_str()
                .to_str()
                .map(str::to_owned)
                .ok_or_else(|| PayloadError::InvalidPayloadPath(path.to_owned()))
        })
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
        .map(|parts| parts.join("/"))
}

fn payload_path(parts: &[&str]) -> String {
    parts
        .iter()
        .map(|part| part.trim_matches('/'))
        .filter(|part| !part.is_empty())
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("/")
}

fn insert_file(
    files: &mut Vec<PayloadFile>,
    relative_path: &str,
    source: &Path,
    reject_duplicate: bool,
) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    if ["py", "pyc", "pyo"]
        .iter()
        .any(|extension| has_extension(source, extension))
    {
        return Err(PayloadError::PythonPayload(source.to_owned()));
    }
    if let Some(existing) = files
        .iter_mut()
        .find(|file| file.relative_path == relative_path)
    {
        if reject_duplicate || existing.kind == PayloadKind::Executable {
            return Err(PayloadError::DuplicateSkillFile(relative_path.to_owned()));
        }
        existing.contents = fs::read(source).map_err(|source_error| PayloadError::Io {
            path: source.to_owned(),
            source: source_error,
        })?;
        return Ok(());
    }
    let contents = fs::read(source).map_err(|source_error| PayloadError::Io {
        path: source.to_owned(),
        source: source_error,
    })?;
    files.push(PayloadFile {
        relative_path: relative_path.to_owned(),
        contents,
        kind: PayloadKind::Data,
    });
    Ok(())
}

fn insert_executable_file(
    files: &mut Vec<PayloadFile>,
    relative_path: &str,
    source: &Path,
) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    if files.iter().any(|file| file.relative_path == relative_path) {
        return Err(PayloadError::DuplicateSkillFile(relative_path.to_owned()));
    }
    let contents = fs::read(source).map_err(|source_error| PayloadError::Io {
        path: source.to_owned(),
        source: source_error,
    })?;
    files.push(PayloadFile {
        relative_path: relative_path.to_owned(),
        contents,
        kind: PayloadKind::Executable,
    });
    Ok(())
}

fn insert_native(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    files: &mut Vec<PayloadFile>,
    binary: &str,
    relative_path: &str,
) -> Result<(), PayloadError> {
    let source = file_path(
        context.sources.as_path(),
        &format!("core/rust/target/release/{binary}"),
    )?;
    let metadata =
        fs::metadata(&source).map_err(|_| PayloadError::MissingNativeBinary(source.clone()))?;
    if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o111 == 0 {
        return Err(PayloadError::MissingNativeBinary(source));
    }
    if files.iter().any(|file| file.relative_path == relative_path) {
        return Err(PayloadError::DuplicateSkillFile(relative_path.to_owned()));
    }
    let contents = fs::read(&source).map_err(|source_error| PayloadError::Io {
        path: source,
        source: source_error,
    })?;
    files.push(PayloadFile {
        relative_path: relative_path.to_owned(),
        contents,
        kind: PayloadKind::Executable,
    });
    Ok(())
}

use std::{fs, io::Write, path::Path, str::FromStr};

use serde_json::{json, Value};
use tempfile::Builder;

use crate::{
    codec::json_bytes,
    error::ProfileManagementError,
    filesystem::file_path,
    model::{ProfileName, RepositoryContext, Version, VersionBump},
    repository::{profile_metadata, profile_names},
};

pub(crate) fn create_profile(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
    description: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(), ProfileManagementError> {
    let profile_name =
        ProfileName::from_str(profile).map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::InvalidProfileName)?;
    if description.is_some_and(|value| value.trim().is_empty()) {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::EmptyDescription);
    }

    let relative = format!("profiles/{}", profile_name.as_str());
    let destination = file_path(&context.sources, &relative)?;
    if destination.exists() {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::ProfileAlreadyExists(
            profile.to_owned(),
        ));
    }
    let parent = destination
        .parent()
        .ok_or(ProfileManagementError::InvalidProfileName)?;
    let temporary = Builder::new()
        .prefix(".create-")
        .tempdir_in(parent)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: parent.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    let staged = temporary.path().join(profile_name.as_str());
    fs::create_dir_all(&staged).map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
        path: staged.clone(),
        source,
    })?;

    let description = description
        .map(str::trim)
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Profil {}", profile_name.as_str()));
    let metadata = json!({
        "name": profile_name.as_str(),
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "description": description,
    });
    write_staged(&staged.join("profile.json"), &json_bytes(&metadata)?)?;
    let mcp = json!({"servers": []});
    write_staged(&staged.join("mcp.json"), &json_bytes(&mcp)?)?;
    write_staged(
        &staged.join("skills.json"),
        &json_bytes(&json!({"skills": []}))?,
    )?;

    fs::create_dir(&destination).map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
        path: destination.clone(),
        source,
    })?;
    let entries = fs::read_dir(&staged).map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::CreateFailed {
        destination: destination.clone(),
    })?;
    for entry in entries {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::CreateFailed {
            destination: destination.clone(),
        })?;
        let target = destination.join(entry.file_name());
        fs::rename(entry.path(), &target).map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::CreateFailed {
            destination: destination.clone(),
        })?;
    }

    println!("OK: utworzono profil {} (1.0.0)", profile_name.as_str());
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn rename_profile(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
    new_name: &str,
) -> Result<(), ProfileManagementError> {
    let old =
        ProfileName::from_str(profile).map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::InvalidProfileName)?;
    let new =
        ProfileName::from_str(new_name).map_err(|_| ProfileManagementError::InvalidProfileName)?;
    if old == new {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::SameProfileName);
    }
    let original = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("profiles/{}", old.as_str()))?;
    let destination = file_path(&context.sources, &format!("profiles/{}", new.as_str()))?;
    if !original.is_dir() {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::UnknownProfile(profile.to_owned()));
    }
    if destination.exists() {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::ProfileAlreadyExists(
            new_name.to_owned(),
        ));
    }
    let metadata_path = file_path(
        &context.sources,
        &format!("profiles/{profile}/profile.json"),
    )?;
    let mut metadata = profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let object = metadata
        .as_object_mut()
        .ok_or_else(|| crate::error::RepositoryError::InvalidMetadata(profile.to_owned()))?;
    object.insert("name".to_owned(), Value::String(new.as_str().to_owned()));
    let bytes = json_bytes(&metadata)?;
    let permissions = fs::metadata(&metadata_path)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: metadata_path.clone(),
            source,
        })?
        .permissions();
    // Stage outside the moved directory so the temporary path remains valid.
    let parent = original
        .parent()
        .ok_or(ProfileManagementError::InvalidProfileName)?;
    let mut temporary = Builder::new()
        .prefix(".rename-")
        .tempfile_in(parent)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: parent.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    temporary
        .write_all(&bytes)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: temporary.path().to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    set_temporary_permissions(&mut temporary, permissions)?;
    fs::rename(&original, &destination).map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
        path: original.clone(),
        source,
    })?;
    if let Err(error) = temporary.persist(destination.join("profile.json")) {
        if let Err(rollback) = fs::rename(&destination, &original) {
            return Err(ProfileManagementError::RenameRollback {
                original,
                destination,
                source: error.error,
                rollback,
            });
        }
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::Persist(error.error.to_string()));
    }
    println!("OK: zmieniono nazwę profilu {profile} -> {new_name} w repozytorium ai-profiles. Zainstalowane projekty nie zostały zmienione.");
    Ok(())
}

fn write_staged(path: &Path, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<(), ProfileManagementError> {
    let mut file = fs::File::create(path).map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
        path: path.to_owned(),
        source,
    })?;
    file.write_all(contents)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: path.to_owned(),
            source,
        })
}

pub(crate) fn bump_profile(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    profile: &str,
    part: VersionBump,
) -> Result<String, ProfileManagementError> {
    if !profile_names(context)?.iter().any(|name| name == profile) {
        return Err(ProfileManagementError::UnknownProfile(profile.to_owned()));
    }
    let relative = format!("profiles/{profile}/profile.json");
    let path = file_path(&context.sources, &relative)?;
    let mut metadata = profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let previous = metadata
        .get("version")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .ok_or_else(|| crate::error::RepositoryError::InvalidMetadata(profile.to_owned()))?
        .to_owned();
    let next = Version::from_str(&previous)?.bump(part).as_string();
    let object = metadata
        .as_object_mut()
        .ok_or_else(|| crate::error::RepositoryError::InvalidMetadata(profile.to_owned()))?;
    object.insert("version".to_owned(), Value::String(next.clone()));

    let parent = path
        .parent()
        .ok_or_else(|| ProfileManagementError::Persist("brak katalogu metadanych".to_owned()))?;
    let mut temporary = Builder::new()
        .prefix(".profile-")
        .suffix(".tmp")
        .tempfile_in(parent)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: parent.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    temporary
        .write_all(&json_bytes(&metadata)?)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: temporary.path().to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    let permissions = fs::metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: path.clone(),
            source,
        })?
        .permissions();
    set_temporary_permissions(&mut temporary, permissions)?;
    temporary
        .persist(&path)
        .map_err(|error| ProfileManagementError::Persist(error.error.to_string()))?;

    println!("OK: {}: {} -> {}", profile, previous, next);
    Ok(next)
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn set_temporary_permissions(
    temporary: &mut tempfile::NamedTempFile,
    permissions: fs::Permissions,
) -> Result<(), ProfileManagementError> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;

    let mode = permissions.mode() & 0o777;
    let permissions = fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode);
    temporary
        .as_file()
        .set_permissions(permissions)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: temporary.path().to_owned(),
            source,
        })
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn set_temporary_permissions(
    temporary: &mut tempfile::NamedTempFile,
    permissions: fs::Permissions,
) -> Result<(), ProfileManagementError> {
    temporary
        .as_file()
        .set_permissions(permissions)
        .map_err(|source| ProfileManagementError::Io {
            path: temporary.path().to_owned(),
            source,
        })
}

//! Client-scoped lifecycle. Preparation is read-only; commits are sequential,
//! deliberately not a cross-client transaction.
use std::{collections::BTreeMap, fs, path::Path, str::FromStr};

use serde_json::{json, Map, Value};

use crate::{
    codec::{json_bytes, sha},
    error::{InstallationError, UninstallError},
    filesystem::{
        check_files, cleanup_empty_directories, file_path, write_bytes, write_executable,
    },
    layout::{Layout, LEGACY_LOCAL, STATE},
    mcp::config::{self, McpConfiguration},
    model::{Client, ClientSelection, ProfileAction, RepositoryContext, Version},
    project::config_for,
    repository::{profile_metadata, source_commit},
};

use super::{
    installation, native,
    payload::{desired_files_for, PayloadFile, PayloadKind},
    uninstall,
};

type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;

struct Manifest {
    clients: Map<String, Value>,
    retained_legacy: Option<Value>,
}

impl Manifest {
    fn load(context: &RepositoryContext, project: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let value = crate::project::manifest_for(context, project)?;
        Ok(Self {
            clients: value["clients"]
                .as_object()
                .ok_or("Brak mapy klientów")?
                .clone(),
            retained_legacy: value.get("retained_legacy").cloned(),
        })
    }

    fn save(&self, project: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        let mut value = json!({"format":2,"clients":self.clients});
        if let Some(retained) = &self.retained_legacy {
            value["retained_legacy"] = retained.clone();
        }
        crate::project::save_manifest(project, &value)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn layout(client: Client, record: &Value) -> Layout {
    Layout::from_state(client, Some(record))
}

/// Render managed command references, not arbitrary values or secrets.
pub(crate) fn render_mcp(servers: &mut Map<String, Value>, layout: Layout) {
    for entry in servers.values_mut() {
        if let Some(command) = entry.get_mut("command").and_then(Value::as_array_mut) {
            for argument in command {
                if let Some(text) = argument.as_str() {
                    if text.starts_with(".opencode/mcp/")
                        || text.starts_with("./.opencode/ai-profile-mcp")
                    {
                        *argument = json!(layout.render(text));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(compose) = entry.pointer_mut("/environment/COMPOSE_FILE") {
            if let Some(text) = compose.as_str() {
                let rendered = text
                    .split(':')
                    .map(|part| {
                        if [
                            "compose.yaml",
                            "compose.host.yaml",
                            "compose.ca.yaml",
                            "compose.auth.yaml",
                            "compose.basic-auth.yaml",
                        ]
                        .iter()
                        .any(|name| part == format!(".opencode/mcp/playwright/{name}"))
                        {
                            layout.managed_path(part)
                        } else {
                            part.to_owned()
                        }
                    })
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join(":");
                *compose = json!(rendered);
            }
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn files_for(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    project: &Path,
    profile: &str,
    client: Client,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<Vec<PayloadFile>> {
    let metadata = profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let layout = Layout::Isolated(client);
    let mut files = desired_files_for(context, profile, client)?;
    for file in &mut files {
        file.relative_path = layout.managed_path(&file.relative_path);
        // Only repository-managed documentation/scripts contain layout references.
        // Native executables and arbitrary binary skill assets stay byte-identical.
        if file.kind == PayloadKind::Data
            && matches!(
                Path::new(&file.relative_path)
                    .extension()
                    .and_then(|s| s.to_str()),
                Some("md" | "sh" | "php" | "yaml")
            )
        {
            if let Ok(text) = std::str::from_utf8(&file.contents) {
                file.contents = layout.render(text).into_bytes();
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(native::project_files_for(
        project,
        metadata
            .get("description")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .unwrap_or(""),
        metadata
            .get("version")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .ok_or("Brak wersji")?,
        old,
        files,
        Some(client),
    )
    .map_err(InstallationError::Native)?)
}

struct Prepared {
    client: Client,
    files: Vec<PayloadFile>,
    writes: BTreeMap<String, Vec<u8>>,
    old: Option<Value>,
    record: Value,
}

fn check_destination(project: &Path, relative: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let path = file_path(project, relative)?;
    if path.exists() && !path.is_file() {
        return Err(InstallationError::DestinationNotFile(relative.into()).into());
    }
    for parent in path.ancestors().skip(1).take_while(|p| *p != project) {
        if parent.exists() && !parent.is_dir() {
            return Err(InstallationError::ParentNotDirectory(parent.into()).into());
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn prepare(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    project: &Path,
    client: Client,
    profile: &str,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<Prepared> {
    let metadata = profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let version = metadata
        .get("version")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .ok_or("Brak wersji")?;
    if let Some(old) = old {
        if old.get("profile").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(profile) {
            if let Some(previous) = old.get("version").and_then(Value::as_str) {
                if Version::from_str(previous)? > Version::from_str(version)? {
                    return Err(InstallationError::Downgrade.into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let target = Layout::Isolated(client);
    let skill_directory = file_path(project, &target.skills())?;
    if skill_directory.exists() && !skill_directory.is_dir() {
        return Err("Cel skills nie jest katalogiem".into());
    }
    let skills_directory_added = old
        .filter(|s| layout(client, s) == target)
        .and_then(|s| s.get("skills_directory_added"))
        .and_then(Value::as_bool)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| !skill_directory.exists());
    let files = files_for(context, project, profile, client, old)?;
    check_files(
        project,
        old,
        files.iter().map(|file| file.relative_path.as_str()),
    )?;
    let mut base = config::definitions_to_map(&config::desired_mcp(context, profile)?);
    render_mcp(&mut base, target);
    let mut writes = BTreeMap::new();
    if base.contains_key("playwright") {
        if let Some(updated) = crate::project::playwright_gitignore_update(project)? {
            writes.insert(".gitignore".to_owned(), updated);
        }
    }
    let legacy = old.is_some_and(|s| layout(client, s) == Layout::Legacy);
    let local = target.local();
    let effective_local = if legacy {
        let source = file_path(project, LEGACY_LOCAL)?;
        let destination = file_path(project, &local)?;
        if source.exists() {
            let bytes = fs::read(&source)?;
            if destination.exists() && fs::read(&destination)? != bytes {
                return Err("Konflikt docelowych local.json; zachowano oba pliki".into());
            }
            if !destination.exists() {
                writes.insert(local.clone(), bytes);
            }
            LEGACY_LOCAL.to_owned()
        } else {
            local.clone()
        }
    } else {
        local.clone()
    };
    let mut resolved = config::resolve_at(project, &base, &effective_local)?;
    if legacy && effective_local == LEGACY_LOCAL {
        render_mcp(&mut resolved.overrides, target);
        resolved = config::resolve_overrides(project, &base, resolved.overrides)?;
        writes.insert(
            local.clone(),
            json_bytes(&json!({"mcp":resolved.overrides}))?,
        );
    }
    let McpConfiguration {
        servers: mcp,
        overrides,
    } = if legacy {
        config::configured_from(project, resolved, &target.adapter())?
    } else {
        config::configured_mcp_at(project, &base, &local, &target.adapter())?
    };
    let mut native_state = Map::new();
    let mut had_config = old
        .and_then(|s| s.get("had_config"))
        .cloned()
        .unwrap_or(json!(false));
    match client {
        Client::OpenCode => {
            let (mut configuration, existed) = config_for(project)?;
            config::check_mcp(
                configuration
                    .as_object()
                    .ok_or("Nieprawidłowa konfiguracja")?,
                old,
                &mcp,
            )?;
            if legacy {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    uninstall::remove_managed_skill_source(
                        &mut configuration,
                        project,
                        old,
                        Layout::Legacy,
                    )?;
                }
            }
            let added = installation::update_project_config(
                &mut configuration,
                old,
                &mcp,
                project,
                target,
            )?;
            native_state.insert("opencode_skill_path_added".into(), json!(added));
            if old.is_none() {
                had_config = json!(existed);
            }
            writes.insert("opencode.json".into(), json_bytes(&configuration)?);
        }
        Client::Claude => {
            let translated = config::native_client_mcp(&mcp, "claude")?;
            let (settings, mut meta) = native::configure_claude(project, old, &mcp)?;
            preserve_original_file_flag(old, &mut meta, "claude", "settings_had_file");
            let (configuration, mcp_meta) =
                native::configure_claude_mcp(project, old, &translated)?;
            writes.insert(".claude/settings.json".into(), settings);
            writes.insert(".mcp.json".into(), configuration);
            native_state.insert("claude".into(), meta);
            native_state.insert("mcp_claude".into(), mcp_meta);
        }
        Client::Codex => {
            let catalog = files
                .iter()
                .find(|file| file.relative_path == ".agents/plugins/marketplace.json")
                .ok_or("Brak katalogu Codex")?;
            let (configuration, mut meta) =
                native::configure_codex(project, old, &catalog.contents)?;
            preserve_original_file_flag(old, &mut meta, "codex", "config_had_file");
            let translated = config::native_client_mcp(&mcp, "codex")?;
            let (configuration, mcp_meta) = native::configure_codex_mcp(
                &configuration,
                old,
                &translated,
                meta.get("config_had_file").and_then(Value::as_bool),
            )?;
            writes.insert(".codex/config.toml".into(), configuration);
            native_state.insert("codex".into(), meta);
            native_state.insert("mcp_codex".into(), mcp_meta);
        }
    }
    for relative in writes
        .keys()
        .map(String::as_str)
        .chain(files.iter().map(|f| f.relative_path.as_str()))
        .chain([STATE])
    {
        check_destination(project, relative)?;
    }
    let hashes: Map<String, Value> = files
        .iter()
        .map(|f| (f.relative_path.clone(), json!(sha(&f.contents))))
        .collect();
    if let Some(prior) = old
        .and_then(|s| s.get("mcp_base"))
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
    {
        installation::print_override_warnings(prior, &base, &overrides);
    }
    let record = json!({"profile":profile,"version":version,"source_commit":source_commit(context),
        "layout":"isolated","files":hashes,"mcp":mcp,"mcp_base":base,"skills_directory_added":skills_directory_added,
        "mcp_local_hash":sha(&json_bytes(&overrides)?),"had_config":had_config,
        "gitignore_added":false,"instructions_added":false,"native":native_state});
    Ok(Prepared {
        client,
        files,
        writes,
        old: old.cloned(),
        record,
    })
}

fn preserve_original_file_flag(old: Option<&Value>, meta: &mut Value, client: &str, key: &str) {
    if let Some(original) = old.and_then(|s| s.pointer(&format!("/native/{client}/{key}"))) {
        meta[key] = original.clone();
    }
}

impl Prepared {
    fn commit(
        mut self,
        project: &Path,
        manifest: &mut Manifest,
        runner: &impl crate::process::ProcessRunner,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        if let Some(old) = &self.old {
            if let Some(files) = old.get("files").and_then(Value::as_object) {
                let removed: Vec<String> = files
                    .keys()
                    .filter(|p| !self.files.iter().any(|f| f.relative_path == **p))
                    .cloned()
                    .collect();
                for relative in &removed {
                    fs::remove_file(file_path(project, relative)?)?;
                }
                cleanup_empty_directories(project, &removed)?;
            }
        }
        for file in &self.files {
            let path = file_path(project, &file.relative_path)?;
            match file.kind {
                PayloadKind::Data => write_bytes(&path, &file.contents)?,
                PayloadKind::Executable => write_executable(&path, &file.contents)?,
            }
        }
        // Native manifests allow an empty skill selection.
        fs::create_dir_all(
            project.join(format!("{}/skills", Layout::Isolated(self.client).plugin())),
        )?;
        for (relative, bytes) in &self.writes {
            native::write_config(project, relative, bytes).map_err(InstallationError::Native)?;
        }
        if self.client == Client::OpenCode {
            if let Some(old) = &self.old {
                crate::project::remove_gitignore_line(project, old)?;
            }
        }
        let key = self.client.as_str();
        let activation = match self.client {
            Client::OpenCode => json!({"activation":"prepared"}),
            Client::Claude => native::activate_claude_with_runner(
                project,
                &self.record["native"][key],
                self.record
                    .pointer("/native/claude/installed_before")
                    .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                    == Some(true),
                runner,
            ),
            Client::Codex => {
                native::activate_selected_codex(project, &self.record["native"][key], runner)
            }
        };
        if self.client != Client::OpenCode {
            let meta = self.record["native"][key]
                .as_object_mut()
                .ok_or("Brak metadanych aktywacji")?;
            meta.insert("activation".into(), activation["activation"].clone());
            for (source, destination) in [
                ("reason", "activation_reason"),
                ("stage", "activation_stage"),
                ("plugin_added", "plugin_added"),
                ("marketplace_added", "marketplace_added"),
            ] {
                if let Some(value) = activation.get(source) {
                    meta.insert(destination.into(), value.clone());
                }
            }
            if activation["activation"] == "synchronized" {
                meta.insert(
                    "last_successful_sync".into(),
                    json!(std::time::SystemTime::now()
                        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?
                        .as_secs()),
                );
                if self.client == Client::Claude {
                    meta.insert("installed_before".into(), json!(true));
                }
            } else if let Some(previous) = self
                .old
                .as_ref()
                .and_then(|s| s.pointer(&format!("/native/{key}/last_successful_sync")))
            {
                meta.insert("last_successful_sync".into(), previous.clone());
            }
        }
        manifest.clients.insert(key.into(), self.record);
        manifest.save(project)?;
        let guide = Layout::Isolated(self.client).readme();
        if project.join(&guide).is_file() {
            println!("Instrukcja konfiguracji: {guide}");
        }
        if activation["activation"] == "pending" || activation["activation"] == "failed" {
            println!(
                "Ponów: make update CLIENT={key} PROJECT=\"{}\"",
                project.display()
            );
        }
        if activation["activation"] == "failed" {
            return Err(
                format!("{key}: aktywacja nie powiodła się; przygotowany stan zachowano").into(),
            );
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub(crate) fn run(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    project: &Path,
    action: ProfileAction,
    selection: ClientSelection,
    profile: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<()> {
    run_with_runner(
        context,
        project,
        action,
        selection,
        profile,
        &native::default_runner(),
    )
}

pub(crate) fn run_with_runner(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    project: &Path,
    action: ProfileAction,
    selection: ClientSelection,
    profile: Option<&str>,
    runner: &impl crate::process::ProcessRunner,
) -> Result<()> {
    let mut manifest = Manifest::load(context, project)?;
    let selected: Vec<Client> = match selection {
        ClientSelection::One(c) => vec![c],
        ClientSelection::All => Client::ALL.to_vec(),
    };
    if action == ProfileAction::Status {
        let mut errors = Vec::new();
        for client in selected {
            if let Err(error) = super::status::status(
                context,
                project,
                client,
                manifest.clients.get(client.as_str()),
            ) {
                eprintln!("{}: {error}", client.as_str());
                errors.push(client.as_str());
            }
        }
        return if errors.is_empty() {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(format!("Błędy statusu: {}", errors.join(", ")).into())
        };
    }
    let mut prepared = Vec::new();
    let mut removals = Vec::new();
    for client in selected {
        let old = manifest.clients.get(client.as_str());
        match action {
            ProfileAction::Install => {
                let profile = profile.ok_or("install wymaga profilu")?;
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    if selection == ClientSelection::All
                        && old.get("profile").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(profile)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                    return Err(format!(
                        "{}: profil już zainstalowany; użyj update albo switch",
                        client.as_str()
                    )
                    .into());
                }
                prepared.push(prepare(context, project, client, profile, None)?);
            }
            ProfileAction::Update | ProfileAction::Switch | ProfileAction::Uninstall => {
                let Some(old) = old else {
                    if selection == ClientSelection::All {
                        continue;
                    }
                    return Err(format!("{}: brak profilu; użyj install", client.as_str()).into());
                };
                if action == ProfileAction::Uninstall {
                    preflight_remove(project, client, old)?;
                    removals.push((client, old.clone()));
                } else {
                    let profile = if action == ProfileAction::Switch {
                        profile
                    } else {
                        old.get("profile").and_then(Value::as_str)
                    }
                    .ok_or("Brak profilu")?;
                    prepared.push(prepare(context, project, client, profile, Some(old))?);
                }
            }
            ProfileAction::Help
            | ProfileAction::Create
            | ProfileAction::Rename
            | ProfileAction::Status
            | ProfileAction::Validate
            | ProfileAction::MigrateLegacy
            | ProfileAction::Bump => return Err("Nieobsługiwana operacja klienta".into()),
        }
    }
    if prepared.is_empty() && removals.is_empty() {
        if action == ProfileAction::Install {
            println!("OK: wszyscy klienci mają wskazany profil; bez zmian");
            return Ok(());
        }
        return Err(UninstallError::NoInstalledProfile.into());
    }
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    for operation in prepared {
        let client = operation.client;
        if let Err(error) = operation.commit(project, &mut manifest, runner) {
            eprintln!("{}: {error}", client.as_str());
            errors.push(client.as_str());
        }
    }
    for (client, old) in removals {
        match remove(project, client, &old) {
            Ok(()) => {
                manifest.clients.remove(client.as_str());
                manifest.save(project)?;
                println!("OK: {} usunięty", client.as_str());
            }
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("{}: {error}", client.as_str());
                errors.push(client.as_str());
            }
        }
    }
    if errors.is_empty() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!(
            "Operacja nie powiodła się dla: {}; pozostałe wyniki zachowano",
            errors.join(", ")
        )
        .into())
    }
}

fn preflight_remove(project: &Path, client: Client, old: &Value) -> Result<()> {
    check_files(project, Some(old), std::iter::empty())?;
    if layout(client, old) != Layout::Legacy {
        file_path(project, &layout(client, old).skills())?;
    }
    inspect_configuration(project, client, old)
}

pub(crate) fn inspect_configuration(project: &Path, client: Client, old: &Value) -> Result<()> {
    let empty = Map::new();
    match client {
        Client::OpenCode => {
            let (config, _) = config_for(project)?;
            config::check_mcp(
                config.as_object().ok_or("Nieprawidłowa konfiguracja")?,
                Some(old),
                &empty,
            )?;
        }
        Client::Claude => {
            native::configure_claude_mcp(project, Some(old), &empty)?;
            native::configure_claude(project, Some(old), &empty).map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
        }
        Client::Codex => {
            let path = file_path(project, ".codex/config.toml")?;
            let bytes = if path.exists() {
                fs::read(path)?
            } else {
                Vec::new()
            };
            native::configure_codex_mcp(&bytes, Some(old), &empty, None)?;
            let catalog = fs::read(file_path(project, ".agents/plugins/marketplace.json")?)?;
            native::configure_codex(project, Some(old), &catalog)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn remove(project: &Path, client: Client, old: &Value) -> Result<()> {
    let empty = Map::new();
    match client {
        Client::OpenCode => {
            let (mut config, _) = config_for(project)?;
            uninstall::remove_managed_mcp(&mut config, old)?;
            uninstall::remove_managed_instruction(&mut config, old)?;
            uninstall::remove_managed_skill_source(&mut config, project, old, layout(client, old))?;
            crate::project::remove_gitignore_line(project, old)?;
            let path = file_path(project, "opencode.json")?;
            if old.get("had_config").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(false)
                && config
                    .as_object()
                    .is_some_and(|o| o.is_empty() || (o.len() == 1 && o.contains_key("$schema")))
            {
                if path.exists() {
                    fs::remove_file(path)?;
                }
            } else {
                write_bytes(&path, &json_bytes(&config)?)?;
            }
        }
        Client::Claude => {
            let (bytes, _) = native::configure_claude_mcp(project, Some(old), &empty)?;
            if let Some(warning) = native::cleanup_claude(project, old) {
                println!("WARNING: {warning}");
            }
            native::remove_claude(project, old)?;
            let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)?;
            let path = file_path(project, ".mcp.json")?;
            if old
                .pointer("/native/mcp_claude/had_file")
                .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                == Some(false)
                && value.as_object().is_some_and(Map::is_empty)
            {
                if path.exists() {
                    fs::remove_file(path)?;
                }
            } else {
                write_bytes(&path, &bytes)?;
            }
        }
        Client::Codex => {
            if let Some(warning) = native::cleanup_codex(project, old) {
                println!("WARNING: {warning}");
            }
            native::remove_codex(project, old)?;
            let path = file_path(project, ".codex/config.toml")?;
            let bytes = if path.exists() {
                fs::read(&path)?
            } else {
                Vec::new()
            };
            let (bytes, _) = native::configure_codex_mcp(&bytes, Some(old), &empty, None)?;
            if old
                .pointer("/native/mcp_codex/config_had_file")
                .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                == Some(false)
                && String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).trim().is_empty()
            {
                if path.exists() {
                    fs::remove_file(path)?;
                }
            } else {
                write_bytes(&path, &bytes)?;
            }
        }
    }
    let files = old
        .get("files")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .ok_or("Brak listy plików")?;
    for relative in files.keys() {
        fs::remove_file(file_path(project, relative)?)?;
    }
    cleanup_empty_directories(project, &files.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>())?;
    if old.get("skills_directory_added").and_then(Value::as_bool) == Some(true) {
        cleanup_empty_directories(
            project,
            &[format!(
                "{}/.directory-cleanup",
                layout(client, old).skills()
            )],
        )?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

use crate::{
    codec::{json_bytes, sha},
    error::StatusError,
    filesystem::file_path,
    layout::Layout,
    mcp::config,
    model::{Client, RepositoryContext, Version},
    repository::profile_metadata,
};
use serde_json::{Map, Value};
use std::{cmp::Ordering, fs, path::Path, str::FromStr};

pub(crate) fn status(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    project: &Path,
    client: Client,
    old: Option<&Value>,
) -> Result<(), StatusError> {
    let key = client.as_str();
    let Some(old) = old else {
        println!("{key}: brak profilu");
        return Ok(());
    };
    let layout = Layout::from_state(client, Some(old));
    let profile = old
        .get("profile")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .ok_or(StatusError::InvalidState)?;
    let metadata = profile_metadata(context, profile)?;
    let available = metadata
        .get("version")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .ok_or(StatusError::InvalidState)?;
    let installed = old.get("version").and_then(Value::as_str);
    let files = old
        .get("files")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .ok_or(StatusError::InvalidState)?;
    let mut modifications = Vec::new();
    for (relative, hash) in files {
        let path = file_path(project, relative)?;
        if !path.is_file()
            || hash.as_str()
                != Some(
                    sha(&fs::read(&path).map_err(|source| StatusError::Io { path, source })?)
                        .as_str(),
                )
        {
            modifications.push(relative.clone());
        }
    }
    let mut base = config::definitions_to_map(&config::desired_mcp(context, profile)?);
    super::scoped::render_mcp(&mut base, layout);
    let content_changed = if layout == Layout::Legacy {
        true
    } else {
        let expected = super::scoped::files_for(context, project, profile, client, Some(old))
            .map_err(|e| StatusError::Native(e.to_string()))?;
        let expected: Map<String, Value> = expected
            .into_iter()
            .map(|f| (f.relative_path, Value::String(sha(&f.contents))))
            .collect();
        files != &expected || old.get("mcp_base") != Some(&Value::Object(base))
    };
    let label = match installed {
        None => "VERSION UNKNOWN (legacy)",
        Some(installed) => {
            match Version::from_str(installed)?.cmp(&Version::from_str(available)?) {
                Ordering::Less => "UPDATE AVAILABLE",
                Ordering::Greater => "INSTALLED NEWER (downgrade blocked)",
                Ordering::Equal if content_changed => "CONTENT CHANGED WITHOUT VERSION BUMP",
                Ordering::Equal => "UP TO DATE",
            }
        }
    };
    println!(
        "{key}: profil {profile}; wersja {}; dostępna {available}; {label}",
        installed.unwrap_or("UNKNOWN")
    );
    println!(
        "{key}: layout {}; lokalne zmiany: {}",
        old["layout"],
        if modifications.is_empty() {
            "brak".into()
        } else {
            modifications.join(", ")
        }
    );
    // Pure reconciliation only. No CLI discovery, registration or activation.
    super::scoped::inspect_configuration(project, client, old)
        .map_err(|e| StatusError::Native(e.to_string()))?;
    let overrides = config::resolve_at(
        project,
        old.get("mcp_base")
            .and_then(Value::as_object)
            .ok_or(StatusError::InvalidState)?,
        &layout.local(),
    )?
    .overrides;
    println!(
        "{key}: nadpisania MCP: {}; połączenie runtime: niesprawdzone",
        overrides.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
    );
    if old.get("mcp_local_hash").and_then(Value::as_str)
        != Some(sha(&json_bytes(&overrides)?).as_str())
    {
        println!(
            "{key}: nadpisania zmienione; make update CLIENT={key} PROJECT=\"{}\"",
            project.display()
        );
    }
    if client != Client::OpenCode {
        let meta = &old["native"][key];
        let disabled = match client {
            Client::Claude => {
                super::native::configure_claude(project, Some(old), &Map::new())
                    .map_err(StatusError::Native)?
                    .1
                    .get("enabled_current")
                    .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                    == Some(false)
            }
            Client::Codex => {
                let path = file_path(project, ".agents/plugins/marketplace.json")?;
                let catalog = fs::read(&path).map_err(|source| StatusError::Io { path, source })?;
                super::native::configure_codex(project, Some(old), &catalog)
                    .map_err(StatusError::Native)?
                    .1
                    .get("activation")
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    == Some("disabled")
            }
            Client::OpenCode => unreachable!("handled above"),
        };
        println!("{key}: aktywacja {}; ostatnia udana synchronizacja {}; ładowanie umiejętności w sesji: niesprawdzone", if disabled { "disabled" } else { meta.get("activation").and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("pending") }, meta["last_successful_sync"]);
    }
    println!(
        "{key}: commit źródłowy {}",
        old.get("source_commit")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .unwrap_or("UNKNOWN")
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

use serde_json::{Map, Value};

use crate::error::UninstallError;
#[cfg(test)]
use crate::{
    codec::json_bytes,
    filesystem::{check_files, cleanup_empty_directories, file_path, write_bytes},
    mcp::config::check_mcp,
    model::RepositoryContext,
    project::{config_for, remove_gitignore_line, state_for},
};

const RULE: &str = ".opencode/workflow-rules.md";
#[cfg(test)]
const GATE: &str = ".opencode/workflow-approval.json";
#[cfg(test)]
const STATE: &str = ".opencode/ai-profile-state.json";
#[cfg(test)]
const SCHEMA: &str = "https://opencode.ai/config.json";

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn uninstall(context: &RepositoryContext, project: &Path) -> Result<(), UninstallError> {
    let old = state_for(context, project)?.ok_or(UninstallError::NoInstalledProfile)?;
    let (mut config, _) = config_for(project)?;
    check_files(project, Some(&old), std::iter::empty())?;
    let config_object = config.as_object().ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    check_mcp(config_object, Some(&old), &Map::new())?;
    crate::profile_management::native::preflight_cleanup(project)
        .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    let empty_native_mcp = Map::new();
    let (claude_mcp_bytes, _) = crate::profile_management::native::configure_claude_mcp(
        project,
        Some(&old),
        &empty_native_mcp,
    )
    .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    let codex_config_path = file_path(project, ".codex/config.toml")?;
    if old.pointer("/native/mcp_codex/entries").is_some() {
        let codex_config_before = if codex_config_path.exists() {
            fs::read(&codex_config_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
                path: codex_config_path.clone(),
                source,
            })?
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };
        let _ = crate::profile_management::native::configure_codex_mcp(
            &codex_config_before,
            Some(&old),
            &empty_native_mcp,
            None,
        )
        .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    }
    if old
        .pointer("/native/claude/mcp_timeout/added")
        .and_then(Value::as_bool)
        == Some(true)
    {
        let settings = file_path(project, ".claude/settings.json")?;
        let value = crate::codec::read_json(&settings)?;
        let current = value.pointer("/env/MCP_TIMEOUT");
        if current != old.pointer("/native/claude/mcp_timeout/expected") {
            return Err(UninstallError::Native(
                "managed Claude env.MCP_TIMEOUT was manually changed".into(),
            ));
        }
    }
    let native_cleanup_warning = crate::profile_management::native::cleanup_claude(project, &old);
    let codex_cleanup_warning = crate::profile_management::native::cleanup_codex(project, &old);
    crate::profile_management::native::remove_claude(project, &old)
        .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    crate::profile_management::native::remove_codex(project, &old)
        .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    if old.pointer("/native/mcp_codex/entries").is_some() {
        let codex_config_after = if codex_config_path.exists() {
            fs::read(&codex_config_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
                path: codex_config_path.clone(),
                source,
            })?
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };
        let (codex_clean, _) = crate::profile_management::native::configure_codex_mcp(
            &codex_config_after,
            Some(&old),
            &empty_native_mcp,
            None,
        )
        .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
        if old
            .pointer("/native/mcp_codex/config_had_file")
            .and_then(Value::as_bool)
            == Some(false)
            && String::from_utf8_lossy(&codex_clean).trim().is_empty()
        {
            if codex_config_path.exists() {
                fs::remove_file(&codex_config_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
                    path: codex_config_path.clone(),
                    source,
                })?;
            }
        } else {
            crate::profile_management::native::write_config(
                project,
                ".codex/config.toml",
                &codex_clean,
            )
            .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
        }
    }
    let claude_mcp_path = file_path(project, ".mcp.json")?;
    let claude_json: Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(&claude_mcp_bytes).map_err(|_| UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    if old
        .pointer("/native/mcp_claude/had_file")
        .and_then(Value::as_bool)
        == Some(false)
        && claude_json.as_object().is_some_and(Map::is_empty)
    {
        if claude_mcp_path.exists() {
            fs::remove_file(&claude_mcp_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
                path: claude_mcp_path,
                source,
            })?;
        }
    } else if old.pointer("/native/mcp_claude/entries").is_some() {
        crate::profile_management::native::write_config(project, ".mcp.json", &claude_mcp_bytes)
            .map_err(UninstallError::Native)?;
    }

    let old_files = old
        .get("files")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    for relative in old_files.keys() {
        let path = file_path(project, relative)?;
        fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io { path, source })?;
    }

    remove_managed_mcp(&mut config, &old)?;
    remove_managed_instruction(&mut config, &old)?;
    remove_managed_skill_source(&mut config, project, &old, crate::layout::Layout::Legacy)?;

    let config_path = file_path(project, "opencode.json")?;
    if !is_truthy(old.get("had_config").unwrap_or(&Value::Null)) && is_schema_only(&config) {
        fs::remove_file(&config_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
            path: config_path,
            source,
        })?;
    } else {
        write_bytes(&config_path, &json_bytes(&config)?)?;
    }

    let gate = file_path(project, GATE)?;
    match fs::remove_file(&gate) {
        Ok(()) => {}
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
        Err(source) => return Err(UninstallError::Io { path: gate, source }),
    }
    let state_path = file_path(project, STATE)?;
    fs::remove_file(&state_path).map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
        path: state_path,
        source,
    })?;
    remove_gitignore_line(project, &old)?;

    let mut cleanup_paths = old_files.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    cleanup_paths.extend([STATE.to_owned(), GATE.to_owned()]);
    cleanup_empty_directories(project, &cleanup_paths)?;

    println!(
        "OK: profil usunięty z {}. Pozostałe pliki projektu zachowane.",
        project.display()
    );
    if let Some(warning) = native_cleanup_warning {
        println!("WARNING: {warning}.");
    }
    if let Some(warning) = codex_cleanup_warning {
        println!("WARNING: {warning}.");
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn remove_managed_skill_source(
    config: &mut Value,
    project: &Path,
    old: &Value,
    layout: crate::layout::Layout,
) -> Result<(), UninstallError> {
    if old
        .pointer("/native/opencode_skill_path_added")
        .and_then(Value::as_bool)
        != Some(true)
    {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let object = config.as_object_mut().ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    let canonical_project = project
        .canonicalize()
        .map_err(|source| UninstallError::Io {
            path: project.to_owned(),
            source,
        })?;
    let source = canonical_project
        .join(layout.skills())
        .to_string_lossy()
        .into_owned();
    let remove_skills = {
        let Some(skills) = object.get_mut("skills") else {
            return Ok(());
        };
        if let Some(paths) = skills.as_array_mut() {
            paths.retain(|entry| entry.as_str() != Some(&source));
            paths.is_empty()
        } else {
            let skills = skills.as_object_mut().ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
            let paths = skills
                .get_mut("paths")
                .and_then(Value::as_array_mut)
                .ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
            paths.retain(|entry| entry.as_str() != Some(&source));
            if paths.is_empty() {
                skills.remove("paths");
            }
            skills.is_empty()
        }
    };
    if remove_skills {
        object.remove("skills");
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn remove_managed_mcp(config: &mut Value, old: &Value) -> Result<(), UninstallError> {
    let object = config.as_object_mut().ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    let mut current = match object.get("mcp") {
        None => Map::new(),
        Some(value) => value
            .as_object()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?,
    };
    let owned = old
        .get("mcp")
        .and_then(Value::as_object)
        .ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    for name in owned.keys() {
        current.shift_remove(name);
    }
    if current.is_empty() {
        object.remove("mcp");
    } else {
        object.insert("mcp".to_owned(), Value::Object(current));
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn remove_managed_instruction(
    config: &mut Value,
    old: &Value,
) -> Result<(), UninstallError> {
    let object = config.as_object_mut().ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?;
    let mut instructions = match object.get("instructions") {
        None => Vec::new(),
        Some(value) => value
            .as_array()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or(UninstallError::InvalidState)?,
    };
    if old.get("instructions_added").is_some_and(is_truthy) {
        instructions.retain(|item| item != RULE);
    }
    if instructions.is_empty() {
        object.remove("instructions");
    } else {
        object.insert("instructions".to_owned(), Value::Array(instructions));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn is_schema_only(config: &Value) -> bool {
    let Some(object) = config.as_object() else {
        return false;
    };
    object.len() == 1 && object.get("$schema").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some(SCHEMA)
}

fn is_truthy(value: &Value) -> bool {
    match value {
        Value::Null => false,
        Value::Bool(value) => *value,
        Value::Number(value) => value.as_f64().is_some_and(|number| number != 0.0),
        Value::String(value) => !value.is_empty(),
        Value::Array(value) => !value.is_empty(),
        Value::Object(value) => !value.is_empty(),
    }
}

use std::fs;

use serde_json::{Map, Value};

use crate::{
    codec::read_json,
    error::ValidationError,
    filesystem::file_path,
    mcp::config::{definitions_to_map, desired_mcp, mcp_definition},
    model::RepositoryContext,
    profile_management::payload::{desired_files, validate_skill_catalog},
    repository::{profile_metadata, profile_names},
};

pub(crate) fn validate_sources(
    context: &RepositoryContext,
    selected_profile: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(), ValidationError> {
    if let Some(profile) = selected_profile {
        if !profile_names(context)?.iter().any(|name| name == profile) {
            return Err(ValidationError::UnknownProfile(profile.to_owned()));
        }
    }

    if selected_profile.is_none() {
        validate_skill_catalog(context)?;
        let registry = file_path(&context.sources, "mcp/servers")?;
        let mut definitions = fs::read_dir(&registry)
            .map_err(|source| ValidationError::Io {
                path: registry.clone(),
                source,
            })?
            .map(|entry| {
                entry
                    .map(|entry| entry.path())
                    .map_err(|source| ValidationError::Io {
                        path: registry.clone(),
                        source,
                    })
            })
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
        definitions.sort();
        for path in definitions {
            if path
                .extension()
                .is_some_and(|extension| extension == "json")
            {
                let name = path
                    .file_stem()
                    .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
                    .ok_or_else(|| ValidationError::InvalidRegistryName(path.clone()))?;
                mcp_definition(context, name)?;
            }
        }
    }

    let profiles = match selected_profile {
        Some(profile) => vec![profile.to_owned()],
        None => profile_names(context)?,
    };
    let mut all_mcp = Map::new();
    for profile in profiles {
        profile_metadata(context, &profile)?;
        let _ = desired_files(context, &profile)?;
        let definitions = desired_mcp(context, &profile)?;
        for (name, definition) in definitions_to_map(&definitions) {
            all_mcp.insert(name, definition);
        }
    }

    let example_path = context.sources.join("mcp/example.json");
    let example = read_json(&example_path)?;
    let Some(example_object) = example.as_object() else {
        return Err(ValidationError::InvalidExample);
    };
    if example_object.len() != 1 {
        return Err(ValidationError::InvalidExample);
    }
    let Some(example_mcp) = example_object.get("mcp").and_then(Value::as_object) else {
        return Err(ValidationError::InvalidExample);
    };
    if example_mcp.values().any(|entry| !entry.is_object()) {
        return Err(ValidationError::InvalidExample);
    }

    if let Some(profile) = selected_profile {
        if all_mcp
            .iter()
            .any(|(name, entry)| example_mcp.get(name) != Some(entry))
        {
            return Err(ValidationError::ScopedExampleMismatch(profile.to_owned()));
        }
        println!("OK: profil {profile}, wybrane skille i definicje MCP są spójne");
        return Ok(());
    }

    if example_mcp != &all_mcp {
        return Err(ValidationError::FullExampleMismatch);
    }
    println!("OK: wszystkie skille i definicje MCP są spójne");
    Ok(())
}
